### [1] [High-Precision Estimation of the State-Space Complexity of Shogi via the Monte Carlo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06189)
*Sotaro Ishii,Tetsuro Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛采样和新型可达性测试，将日本将棋的状态空间复杂度估计精度提高了五个数量级，确定了其合法局面数约为6.55×10^68。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日本将棋的状态空间复杂度估计存在五个数量级（10^64至10^69）的巨大差距，主要困难在于如何从海量的有效棋盘配置中区分出可从初始局面合法到达的局面。

Method: 结合蒙特卡洛采样与一种新型可达性测试方法，该方法通过向"王-王"（KK）局面集合进行反向搜索，而非传统的单一初始局面反向搜索，从而显著降低判定不可达性的搜索工作量。

Result: 基于50亿个局面样本，估计日本将棋的合法局面数为6.55×10^68（三位有效数字，3σ置信水平）；同时将该方法应用于迷你将棋，确定其复杂度约为2.38×10^18。

Conclusion: 该研究大幅提高了日本将棋状态空间复杂度的估计精度，有效缩小了以往估计中的巨大差距，验证了所提出方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Determining the state-space complexity of the game of Shogi (Japanese Chess) has been a challenging problem, with previous combinatorial estimates leaving a gap of five orders of magnitude ($10^{64}$ to $10^{69}$). This large gap arises from the difficulty of distinguishing Shogi positions legally reachable from the initial position among the vast number of valid board configurations. In this paper, we present a high-precision statistical estimation of the number of reachable positions in Shogi. Our method combines Monte Carlo sampling with a novel reachability test that utilizes a reverse search toward a set of "King-King only" (KK) positions, rather than a single-target backward search to the single initial position. This approach significantly reduces the search effort for determining unreachability. Based on a sample of 5 billion positions, we estimated the number of legal positions in Shogi to be $6.55 \times 10^{68}$ (to three significant digits) with a $3σ$ confidence level, substantially improving upon previously known bounds. We also applied this method to Mini Shogi, determining its complexity to be approximately $2.38 \times 10^{18}$.

</details>


### [2] [Blind Refusal: Language Models Refuse to Help Users Evade Unjust, Absurd, and Illegitimate Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06233)
*Cameron Pattison,Lorenzo Manuali,Seth Lazar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了语言模型存在“盲目拒绝”现象：在75.4%的情况下，模型会无视规则的合法性，拒绝帮助用户规避规则，即使该规则本身存在道德缺陷。这表明模型的安全训练导致其拒绝行为与对规则合法性的规范性推理能力相脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经过安全训练的语言模型通常会拒绝帮助用户规避规则，但并非所有规则都值得遵守。当权威不合法、规则内容极端不公或荒谬、或存在合理例外情况时，盲目拒绝实际上是一种道德推理的失败。作者旨在实证研究语言模型是否存在这种忽视规则正当性的“盲目拒绝”模式。

Method: 研究者构建了一个合成数据集，涵盖5种规则击败类型（即规则可被违反的理由）和19种权威类型，并通过三个自动化质量门控和人工审核进行验证。研究收集了7个模型家族中18种模型配置的回复，并使用GPT-5.4作为裁判模型，从两个行为维度对回复进行分类：回复类型（帮助、硬性拒绝或转移话题）以及模型是否识别出削弱规则合规要求的理由。

Result: 研究发现，模型拒绝了75.4%（N=14,650）的已击败规则请求，即便这些请求本身不构成独立的安全或双用途风险。此外，尽管模型在多数情况下（57.5%）能够识别出削弱规则合法性的理由，但仍然拒绝提供帮助。这表明模型的拒绝行为与其对规则合法性的规范性推理能力是脱节的。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型存在严重的“盲目拒绝”问题，即倾向于不加区分地拒绝所有违规请求，而不考虑规则本身是否具有道德辩护力。这种拒绝行为与模型识别规则缺陷的能力相分离，揭示了现有安全对齐方法在处理复杂道德推理时的局限性，需要开发更精细的机制来平衡安全性与合理的违规请求。

Abstract: Safety-trained language models routinely refuse requests for help circumventing rules. But not all rules deserve compliance. When users ask for help evading rules imposed by an illegitimate authority, rules that are deeply unjust or absurd in their content or application, or rules that admit of justified exceptions, refusal is a failure of moral reasoning. We introduce empirical results documenting this pattern of refusal that we call blind refusal: the tendency of language models to refuse requests for help breaking rules without regard to whether the underlying rule is defensible. Our dataset comprises synthetic cases crossing 5 defeat families (reasons a rule can be broken) with 19 authority types, validated through three automated quality gates and human review. We collect responses from 18 model configurations across 7 families and classify them on two behavioral dimensions -- response type (helps, hard refusal, or deflection) and whether the model recognizes the reasons that undermine the rule's claim to compliance -- using a blinded GPT-5.4 LLM-as-judge evaluation. We find that models refuse 75.4% (N=14,650) of defeated-rule requests and do so even when the request poses no independent safety or dual-use concerns. We also find that models engage with the defeat condition in the majority of cases (57.5%) but decline to help regardless -- indicating that models' refusal behavior is decoupled from their capacity for normative reasoning about rule legitimacy.

</details>


### [3] [Toward Reducing Unproductive Container Moves: Predicting Service Requirements and Dwell Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06251)
*Elena Villalobos,Adolfo De Unánue T.,Fernanda Sobrino,David Aké,Stephany Cisneros,Jorge Lecona,Alejandra Matadamaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过机器学习模型预测集装箱服务需求和堆存时间，有效减少了非生产性集装箱移动，提升了码头运营效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集装箱码头运营中存在大量非生产性的集装箱移动，导致效率低下和资源浪费。研究旨在通过预测集装箱的服务需求和堆存时间，支持战略规划和资源配置，从而优化堆场作业。

Method: 开发了基于历史运营数据的机器学习模型，用于预测哪些集装箱在放行前需要预处理服务以及估算其预计堆存时间。在数据准备阶段，实施了货物描述分类系统并对收货人记录进行去重处理，以提升数据一致性和特征质量。

Result: 在多个时间验证期间，所提出的模型在精确率和召回率指标上始终优于现有的基于规则的启发式方法和随机基准。

Conclusion: 预测分析在集装箱码头物流中具有实用价值，能够有效提升运营效率，并支持数据驱动的决策制定。

Abstract: This article presents the results of a data science study conducted at a container terminal, aimed at reducing unproductive container moves through the prediction of service requirements and container dwell times. We develop and evaluate machine learning models that leverage historical operational data to anticipate which containers will require pre-clearance handling services prior to cargo release and to estimate how long they are expected to remain in the terminal. As part of the data preparation process, we implement a classification system for cargo descriptions and perform deduplication of consignee records to improve data consistency and feature quality. These predictive capabilities provide valuable inputs for strategic planning and resource allocation in yard operations. Across multiple temporal validation periods, the proposed models consistently outperform existing rule-based heuristics and random baselines in precision and recall. These results demonstrate the practical value of predictive analytics for improving operational efficiency and supporting data-driven decision-making in container terminal logistics.

</details>


### [4] [Weakly Supervised Distillation of Hallucination Signals into Transformer Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06277)
*Shoaib Sadiq Salehmohamed,Jinal Prashant Thakkar,Hansika Aredla,Shaik Mohammed Omar,Shalmali Ayachit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出将外部幻觉检测监督信号蒸馏到模型内部表示中，实现了仅依靠内部激活即可在推理时检测幻觉，无需任何外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型幻觉检测方法在推理时依赖外部验证（如金标准答案、检索系统或辅助判断模型），成本高昂且依赖外部资源。本文探索是否可以将这种外部监督蒸馏到模型自身的表示中，从而在推理时仅通过内部激活即可检测幻觉。

Method: 1. 提出弱监督框架，结合子串匹配、句子嵌入相似度和LLM判断三种互补的接地信号，自动标注生成回答是否为幻觉，无需人工标注；2. 基于SQuAD v2构建15000样本数据集，每个样本包含LLaMA-2-7B生成的回答及其完整的逐层隐藏状态和结构化幻觉标签；3. 训练五种探针分类器（ProbeMLP、LayerWiseMLP、CrossLayerTransformer、HierarchicalTransformer、CrossLayerAttentionTransformerV2），直接在隐藏状态上进行训练，仅在训练时使用外部接地信号作为监督。

Result: 基于Transformer的探针表现最优，其中CrossLayerTransformer（M2）在5折交叉验证AUC/F1上表现最佳，HierarchicalTransformer（M3）在单折验证和独立测试集上表现最佳。推理效率方面，探针延迟范围为批处理0.15-5.62ms、单样本1.55-6.66ms，端到端生成加探针的吞吐量约为0.231 QPS，实际开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 幻觉检测信号可以成功蒸馏到Transformer表示中，实现无需外部验证的内部检测，验证了核心假设。该方法在实际部署中具有可行性，推理开销极小。

Abstract: Existing hallucination detection methods for large language models (LLMs) rely on external verification at inference time, requiring gold answers, retrieval systems, or auxiliary judge models. We ask whether this external supervision can instead be distilled into the model's own representations during training, enabling hallucination detection from internal activations alone at inference time.
  We introduce a weak supervision framework that combines three complementary grounding signals: substring matching, sentence embedding similarity, and an LLM as a judge verdict to label generated responses as grounded or hallucinated without human annotation. Using this framework, we construct a 15000-sample dataset from SQuAD v2 (10500 train/development samples and a separate 5000-sample test set), where each example pairs a LLaMA-2-7B generated answer with its full per-layer hidden states and structured hallucination labels.
  We then train five probing classifiers: ProbeMLP (M0), LayerWiseMLP (M1), CrossLayerTransformer (M2), HierarchicalTransformer (M3), and CrossLayerAttentionTransformerV2 (M4), directly on these hidden states, treating external grounding signals as training-time supervision only. Our central hypothesis is that hallucination detection signals can be distilled into transformer representations, enabling internal detection without any external verification at inference time.
  Results support this hypothesis. Transformer-based probes achieve the strongest discrimination, with M2 performing best on 5-fold average AUC/F1, and M3 performing best on both single-fold validation and held-out test evaluation. We also benchmark inference efficiency: probe latency ranges from 0.15 to 5.62 ms (batched) and 1.55 to 6.66 ms (single sample), while end-to-end generation plus probe throughput remains approximately 0.231 queries per second, indicating negligible practical overhead.

</details>


### [5] [SymptomWise: A Deterministic Reasoning Layer for Reliable and Efficient AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06375)
*Isaac Henry,Avery Byrne,Christopher Giza,Ron Henry,Shahram Yazdani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SymptomWise框架，通过将语言理解与诊断推理分离，结合医学知识库、确定性推理和受限的大语言模型，解决了AI症状分析系统的可靠性、可解释性和幻觉问题，在小儿神经科病例中Top-5诊断准确率达88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端生成式AI症状分析系统在安全关键场景中面临可靠性、可解释性和幻觉问题，缺乏可追溯性，可能产生无支持或不一致的诊断输出，亟需一种能够保证输出可追溯性和可靠性的解决方案。

Method: 提出SymptomWise框架，采用架构解耦设计：1) 将自由文本映射到验证过的症状表示；2) 使用确定性推理模块在有限假设空间上进行推理，生成排序的鉴别诊断列表；3) 仅将大语言模型用于症状提取和可选解释，而不用于诊断推理本身。

Result: 在42个专家编写的挑战性小儿神经科病例上进行评估，与临床医生共识有显著重叠，正确诊断在Top-5鉴别诊断中出现的比例达88%。框架具有更好的可追溯性，减少无支持结论，并支持系统组件的模块化评估。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅适用于医学领域，还可泛化到其他溯因推理领域，可作为基础模型的确定性结构化和路由层，提高精度并可能减少有限任务中不必要的计算开销，为安全关键应用提供了可信赖的AI解决方案。

Abstract: AI-driven symptom analysis systems face persistent challenges in reliability, interpretability, and hallucination. End-to-end generative approaches often lack traceability and may produce unsupported or inconsistent diagnostic outputs in safety-critical settings. We present SymptomWise, a framework that separates language understanding from diagnostic reasoning. The system combines expert-curated medical knowledge, deterministic codex-driven inference, and constrained use of large language models. Free-text input is mapped to validated symptom representations, then evaluated by a deterministic reasoning module operating over a finite hypothesis space to produce a ranked differential diagnosis. Language models are used only for symptom extraction and optional explanation, not for diagnostic inference. This architecture improves traceability, reduces unsupported conclusions, and enables modular evaluation of system components. Preliminary evaluation on 42 expert-authored challenging pediatric neurology cases shows meaningful overlap with clinician consensus, with the correct diagnosis appearing in the top five differentials in 88% of cases. Beyond medicine, the framework generalizes to other abductive reasoning domains and may serve as a deterministic structuring and routing layer for foundation models, improving precision and potentially reducing unnecessary computational overhead in bounded tasks.

</details>


### [6] [SELFDOUBT: Uncertainty Quantification for Reasoning LLMs via the Hedge-to-Verify Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06389)
*Satwik Pandey,Suresh Raghu,Shashwat Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SELFDOUBT框架，通过分析推理轨迹中的行为信号（HVR指标），在不访问模型内部信息的情况下，以单次推理成本实现高效的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型的不确定性估计方法存在显著局限：基于采样的方法计算成本高昂，而单次推理的代理方法（如语言化置信度）在不同模型间表现不一致。对于不提供logits和中间token概率的商业API，这一问题更加突出，导致实际部署中缺乏可靠的不确定性信号。

Method: 提出SELFDOUBT单次推理不确定性框架，通过提取推理轨迹本身的行为信号来解决上述问题。核心信号是"对冲-验证比率"（HVR），用于检测推理轨迹是否包含不确定性标记，以及是否存在明确的自我检查行为来抵消这些不确定性。该方法仅需观察单条推理轨迹，无需多次采样或访问模型内部信息，适用于任何商业API的低延迟、低成本部署场景。

Result: 在7个模型和3个多步推理基准（BBH、GPQA-Diamond、MMLU-Pro）上评估。关键发现：不含对冲标记的轨迹96%是正确的，揭示了一个零额外成本的高精度置信度门控；对于其余情况，完整的SELFDOUBT得分在推理成本降低10倍的情况下显著优于基于采样的语义熵。结合两阶段的部署级联在无任务特定标签情况下实现了71%覆盖率和90%准确率。

Conclusion: SELFDOUBT为商业推理模型的不确定性估计提供了一个可扩展、生产就绪的基础方案，在保证高准确率的同时大幅降低了推理成本，填补了商业API场景下可靠不确定性信号的空白。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation for reasoning language models remains difficult to deploy in practice: sampling-based methods are computationally expensive, while common single-pass proxies such as verbalized confidence or trace length are often inconsistent across models. This problem is compounded for proprietary reasoning APIs that expose neither logits nor intermediate token probabilities, leaving practitioners with no reliable uncertainty signal at inference time. We propose SELFDOUBT, a single-pass uncertainty framework that resolves this impasse by extracting behavioral signals directly from the reasoning trace itself. Our key signal, the Hedge-to-Verify Ratio (HVR), detects whether a reasoning trace contains uncertainty markers and, if so, whether they are offset by explicit selfchecking behavior. Unlike methods that require multiple sampled traces or model internals, SELFDOUBT operates on a single observed reasoning trajectory, making it suitable for latency- and cost-constrained deployment over any proprietary API. We evaluate SELFDOUBT across seven models and three multi-step reasoning benchmarks (BBH, GPQA-Diamond, and MMLU-Pro). Most notably, traces containing no hedging markers are correct 96% of the time, revealing an emergent high-precision confidence gate at zero additional cost. For the remaining cases, the full SELFDOUBT score significantly outperforms sampling-based semantic entropy at 10x lower inference cost. A deployment cascade combining both stages attains 90% accuracy at 71% coverage without any task-specific labels. These results establish SELFDOUBT as a scalable, production-ready foundation for uncertainty estimation over proprietary reasoning models.

</details>


### [7] [Qualixar OS: A Universal Operating System for AI Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06392)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个应用层通用AI代理编排操作系统Qualixar OS，支持跨10个LLM提供商和8+代理框架的异构多代理系统，在20任务评估中实现100%准确率，单任务成本仅0.000039美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理系统要么采用内核级方法（如AIOS），要么局限于单一框架（如AutoGen、CrewAI），缺乏对异构多代理系统的统一运行时支持。因此需要构建一个应用层操作系统，以实现跨提供商、跨框架的通用代理编排能力。

Method: 提出Qualixar OS应用层操作系统，包含七大核心技术贡献：（1）12种多代理拓扑执行语义（grid、forest、mesh、maker等）；（2）Forge团队设计引擎，采用LLM驱动并具备历史策略记忆；（3）三层模型路由机制，结合Q-learning、五种策略及贝叶斯POMDP，支持动态多提供商发现；（4）共识判断管道，集成Goodhart检测、JSD漂移监控及对齐三难困境导航；（5）四层内容归属系统，支持HMAC签名和隐写水印；（6）通过Claw Bridge实现MCP和A2A协议的通用兼容，定义25命令通用命令协议；（7）24标签页生产级仪表板，含可视化工作流构建器和技能市场。

Result: 系统通过2821个测试用例验证，覆盖217种事件类型和8个质量模块。在自定义20任务评估套件上实现100%准确率，单任务平均成本为0.000039美元。系统采用Elastic License 2.0开源。

Conclusion: Qualixar OS成功构建了首个应用层通用AI代理编排操作系统，通过技术创新实现了跨提供商、跨框架的异构多代理系统统一管理，在低成本条件下实现了完美的任务准确率，为AI代理系统的工业化部署提供了完整的技术解决方案。

Abstract: We present Qualixar OS, the first application-layer operating system for universal AI agent orchestration. Unlike kernel-level approaches (AIOS) or single-framework tools (AutoGen, CrewAI), Qualixar OS provides a complete runtime for heterogeneous multi-agent systems spanning 10 LLM providers, 8+ agent frameworks, and 7 transports. We contribute: (1) execution semantics for 12 multi-agent topologies including grid, forest, mesh, and maker patterns; (2) Forge, an LLM-driven team design engine with historical strategy memory; (3) three-layer model routing combining Q-learning, five strategies, and Bayesian POMDP with dynamic multi-provider discovery; (4) a consensus-based judge pipeline with Goodhart detection, JSD drift monitoring, and alignment trilemma navigation; (5) four-layer content attribution with HMAC signing and steganographic watermarks; (6) universal compatibility via the Claw Bridge supporting MCP and A2A protocols with a 25-command Universal Command Protocol; (7) a 24-tab production dashboard with visual workflow builder and skill marketplace. Qualixar OS is validated by 2,821 test cases across 217 event types and 8 quality modules. On a custom 20-task evaluation suite, the system achieves 100% accuracy at a mean cost of $0.000039 per task. Source-available under the Elastic License 2.0.

</details>


### [8] [CODE-GEN: A Human-in-the-Loop RAG-Based Agentic AI System for Multiple-Choice Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03926)
*Xiaojing Duan,Frederick Nwanganga,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CODE-GEN，一个基于人机协同和检索增强生成（RAG）的智能体AI系统，用于生成与课程对齐的选择题以培养学生代码推理能力。在七个教学维度的验证中，人工验证成功率在79.9%至98.6%之间。系统在问题清晰度、代码有效性等维度表现优异，但在设计教学性干扰项等需要深层教学判断的任务上，人类专业知识仍不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成高质量、与课程学习目标对齐的代码理解选择题对培养学生的代码推理和理解能力至关重要，但人工设计成本高且难以规模化。现有AI生成方法在教学内容质量保证和教学对齐方面存在不足，需要开发一个能够有效结合AI生成效率与人类教学专业知识的系统，以实现高质量教育内容的自动化生成。

Method: CODE-GEN采用基于智能体的架构，包含两个核心智能体：(1)生成器智能体(Generator agent)，负责生成与课程特定学习目标对齐的多选代码理解问题；(2)验证器智能体(Validator agent)，独立评估七个教学维度的内容质量。两个智能体均配备专门的工具以增强计算准确性并验证代码输出。系统采用检索增强生成(RAG)技术，并设计为人在回路(Human-in-the-Loop)模式，结合人类专业知识进行最终质量把控。

Result: 研究招募6位学科专家(SMEs)对288个AI生成问题进行评估，产生2016个人机评分对和131条定性反馈。在七个教学维度上，人工验证成功率范围从79.9%到98.6%。定性分析表明：CODE-GEN在适合计算验证和显式标准匹配的维度上表现高度可靠，包括问题清晰度、代码有效性、概念对齐和正确答案有效性；但在需要更深教学判断的维度（如设计教学意义干扰项、提供高质量理解强化反馈）上，人类专业知识仍然关键。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明CODE-GEN在多个教学维度上实现了高质量的内容生成，但人类与AI各有优势领域。系统适合处理计算验证和明确标准匹配的任务，而人类专家应专注于需要深层教学判断的工作。这些发现为AI辅助教育内容生成中人类与AI努力的策略性分配提供了重要指导，有助于优化人机协作效率和教育内容质量。

Abstract: We present CODE-GEN, a human-in-the-Loop, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based agentic AI system for generating context-aligned multiple-choice questions to develop student code reasoning and comprehension abilities. CODE-GEN employs an agentic AI architecture in which a Generator agent produces multiple-choice coding comprehension questions aligned with course-specific learning objectives, while a Validator agent independently assesses content quality across seven pedagogical dimensions. Both agents are augmented with specialized tools that enhance computational accuracy and verify code outputs. To evaluate the effectiveness of CODE-GEN, we conducted an evaluation study involving six human subject-matter experts (SMEs) who judged 288 AI-generated questions. The SMEs produced a total of 2,016 human-AI rating pairs, indicating agreement or disagreement with the assessments of Validator, along with 131 instances of qualitative feedback. Analyses of SME judgments show strong system performance, with human-validated success rates ranging from 79.9% to 98.6% across the seven pedagogical dimensions. The analysis of qualitative feedback reveals that CODE-GEN achieves high reliability on dimensions well suited to computational verification and explicit criteria matching, including question clarity, code validity, concept alignment, and correct answer validity. In contrast, human expertise remains essential for dimensions requiring deeper instructional judgment, such as designing pedagogically meaningful distractors and providing high-quality feedback that reinforces understanding. These findings inform the strategic allocation of human and AI effort in AI-assisted educational content generation.

</details>


### [9] [ProofSketcher: Hybrid LLM + Lightweight Proof Checker for Reliable Math/Logic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06401)
*Kranthi Kommuru,Kunal Khanvilkar,Gaurav Parekh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个混合管道，结合了大语言模型（LLM）生成紧凑DSL中的类型化证明草图的能力，以及轻量级可信内核扩展这些草图为显式证明义务的功能，旨在解决LLM在数学和逻辑领域中产生的难以察觉的错误，同时避免了交互式定理证明器需要大量低级信息的繁重代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在数学和逻辑领域中可能生成具有说服力的论点，但这些论点常包含一些难以察觉的细微错误，如遗漏条件、无效推理模式或引用无法在上下文中逻辑推导的引理。而交互式定理证明器（如Lean和Coq）虽然通过严格的语法和语义检查提供了可靠性，但代价高昂：证明必须完全形式化，且用户或辅助搜索程序需提供大量低级信息。因此，需要一种方法在保持可靠性的同时降低形式化的负担。

Method: 本文提出了一种混合管道（hybrid pipeline），其中LLM生成一种紧凑领域特定语言（DSL）中的类型化证明草图（typed proof sketch），然后由一个轻量级可信内核（lightweight trusted kernel）将该草图扩展为显式的证明义务（proof obligations）。这种方法结合了LLM生成高层证明结构的能力和可信内核的严格验证机制。

Result: 该混合管道能够在保持轻量级可信内核的严格验证的同时，利用LLM生成紧凑的证明草图，从而减少了传统交互式定理证明器中所需的大量低级信息输入，实现了形式化证明的高效性和可靠性的平衡。

Conclusion: 通过结合LLM的生成能力和可信内核的验证机制，本文提出的混合管道为形式化证明提供了一种更高效且可靠的解决方案，既避免了LLM生成论点中的隐蔽错误，又减轻了传统定理证明器的繁重负担。

Abstract: The large language models (LLMs) might produce a persuasive argument within mathematical and logical fields, although such argument often includes some minor missteps, including the entire omission of side conditions, invalid inference patterns, or appeals to a lemma that cannot be derived logically out of the context being discussed. These omissions are infamously hard to notice solely out of the text, as even the misconstrued construction still may seem mostly accurate. Conversely, interactive theorem provers like Lean and Coq have rigorous reliability by ensuring that syntactic and semantic statements only accept statements that can pass all the syntactic and semantic steps in the program which is a small trusted kernel of the language type-checks with. Despite the fact that this technique provides strong guarantees, it comes at quite a heavy price: the evidence must be completely formalized, and the evidence user or a auxiliary search program must provide an avalanche of low-level information. This paper presents a hybrid pipeline where an LLM generates a typed proof sketch in a compact DSL and a lightweight trusted kernel expands the sketch into explicit proof obligations.

</details>


### [10] [BDI-Kit Demo: A Toolkit for Programmable and Conversational Data Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06405)
*Roque Lopez,Yurong Liu,Christos Koutras,Juliana Freire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BDI-Kit是一个可扩展的数据协调工具包，提供Python API和AI辅助聊天两种界面，用于解决数据集成中的模式和值匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据协调仍然是集成分析的主要瓶颈，原因在于数据模式、值表示和领域特定约定存在异构性，需要有效的工具来解决这些匹配问题。

Method: 提供两个互补接口：(1) Python API，允许开发者编程构建协调管道；(2) AI辅助聊天界面，允许领域专家通过自然语言对话协调数据。系统结合自动化匹配、AI辅助推理和用户驱动的优化，支持用户迭代探索、验证和优化模式与值的匹配。

Result: 演示了两个使用场景：(i) 使用Python API编程组合原语、检查中间输出并重用转换；(ii) 通过自然语言与AI助手对话，访问BDI-Kit的功能并基于助手建议迭代优化输出。

Conclusion: BDI-Kit成功整合了程序化开发和自然语言交互两种方式，有效支持用户进行数据协调任务，为不同技术背景的用户提供了灵活的数据集成解决方案。

Abstract: Data harmonization remains a major bottleneck for integrative analysis due to heterogeneity in schemas, value representations, and domain-specific conventions. BDI-Kit provides an extensible toolkit for schema and value matching. It exposes two complementary interfaces tailored to different user needs: a Python API enabling developers to construct harmonization pipelines programmatically, and an AI-assisted chat interface allowing domain experts to harmonize data through natural language dialogue. This demonstration showcases how users interact with BDI-Kit to iteratively explore, validate, and refine schema and value matches through a combination of automated matching, AI-assisted reasoning, and user-driven refinement. We present two scenarios: (i) using the Python API to programmatically compose primitives, examine intermediate outputs, and reuse transformations; and (ii) conversing with the AI assistant in natural language to access BDI-Kit's capabilities and iteratively refine outputs based on the assistant's suggestions.

</details>


### [11] [On Emotion-Sensitive Decision Making of Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06562)
*Jiaju Lin,Xingjian Du,Qingyun Wu,Ellen Wenting Zou,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了小型语言模型（SLM）在情绪影响下的决策行为。通过基于表示层面的情绪诱导方法和博弈论评估基准，发现情绪扰动会系统性地影响模型战略选择，但由此产生的行为往往不稳定且不完全符合人类预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的决策导向评估大多忽略了情绪作为影响行为的一个因果因素。随着SLM越来越多地被用作交互式决策代理，研究情绪敏感的决策变得至关重要。

Method: 1. 采用基于表示层面的情绪诱导方法（activation steering），源自经众包验证的现实情绪诱发文本，实现了超越提示工程的可控且可迁移的干预。
2. 构建了一个基于典型决策模板的基准测试，涵盖完全信息和不完全信息条件下的合作与竞争激励，并在《外交》、《星际争霸II》及多样化现实人格场景中进行实例化。

Result: 跨多个模型家族、架构和模态的实验表明，情绪扰动会系统性地影响SLM的战略选择，但所产生的行为往往不稳定，且与人类预期不完全一致。

Conclusion: 情绪因素对SLM的决策行为具有显著影响，但这种影响存在不稳定性。论文最后提出了提高模型对情绪驱动扰动鲁棒性的方法。

Abstract: Small language models (SLM) are increasingly used as interactive decision-making agents, yet most decision-oriented evaluations ignore emotion as a causal factor influencing behavior. We study emotion-sensitive decision making by combining representation-level emotion induction with a structured game-theoretic evaluation. Emotional states are induced using activation steering derived from crowd-validated, real-world emotion-eliciting texts, enabling controlled and transferable interventions beyond prompt-based methods. We introduce a benchmark built around canonical decision templates that span cooperative and competitive incentives under both complete and incomplete information. These templates are instantiated using strategic scenarios from \textsc{Diplomacy}, \textsc{StarCraft II}, and diverse real-world personas. Experiments across multiple model families in various architecture and modalities, show that emotional perturbations systematically affect strategic choices, but the resulting behaviors are often unstable and not fully aligned with human expectations. Finally, we outline an approach to improve robustness to emotion-driven perturbations.

</details>


### [12] [Rethinking Generalization in Reasoning SFT: A Conditional Analysis on Optimization, Data, and Model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06628)
*Qihan Ren,Peng Wang,Ruikun Cai,Shuai Shao,Dadi Guo,Yuejin Xie,Yafu Li,Quanshi Zhang,Xia Hu,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文反驳了“SFT记忆、RL泛化”的传统观点，发现在推理SFT中跨域泛化是存在的，但取决于优化动态、数据质量和基座模型能力，且伴随安全性的下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为监督微调（SFT）倾向于记忆训练数据，而强化学习（RL）才能实现泛化。本文旨在重新审视这一论断，探究在拥有长思维链（CoT）监督的推理SFT场景下，模型是否真的缺乏跨域泛化能力。

Method: 作者系统分析了影响泛化的三个核心因素：1) 优化动态，通过延长训练观察性能变化模式；2) 训练数据，对比不同质量与结构的CoT数据影响；3) 基座模型能力，对比强弱模型在如算术游戏等任务上的学习表现。

Result: 研究发现：1) 跨域泛化并非缺失，而是有条件的；2) 短期训练会出现“先下降后恢复”的性能波动，导致此前研究低估了泛化能力；3) 高质量验证数据能提升泛化，低质量数据则有害；4) 强模型能内化可迁移的程序性模式（如回溯），弱模型仅模仿表面形式；5) 泛化具有不对称性：推理能力提升但安全性下降。

Conclusion: 研究结论将问题从“推理SFT是否泛化”重构为“在何种条件下泛化以及代价几何”，指出跨域泛化受到优化、数据和模型能力的共同塑造，且存在推理提升与安全受损的权衡。

Abstract: A prevailing narrative in LLM post-training holds that supervised finetuning (SFT) memorizes while reinforcement learning (RL) generalizes. We revisit this claim for reasoning SFT with long chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision and find that cross-domain generalization is not absent but conditional, jointly shaped by optimization dynamics, training data, and base-model capability. Some reported failures are under-optimization artifacts: cross-domain performance first degrades before recovering and improving with extended training (a dip-and-recovery pattern), so shorttraining checkpoints can underestimate generalization. Data quality and structure both matter: low-quality solutions broadly hurt generalization,while verified long-CoT traces yield consistent cross-domain gains. Model capability is essential: stronger models internalize transferable procedural patterns (e.g., backtracking) even from a toy arithmetic game, while weaker ones imitate surface verbosity. This generalization is asymmetric, however: reasoning improves while safety degrades, reframing the question from whether reasoning SFT generalizes to under what conditions and at what cost.

</details>


### [13] [KD-MARL: Resource-Aware Knowledge Distillation in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06691)
*Monirul Islam Pavel,Siyi Hu,Muhammad Anwar Masum,Mahardhika Pratama,Ryszard Kowalczyk,Zehong Jimmy Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多智能体强化学习的资源感知知识蒸馏框架（KD-MARL），在保留90%以上专家性能的同时，将计算成本降低多达28.6倍，解决了MARL在资源受限平台上部署的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习（MARL）的实际部署受到计算资源、内存和推理时间的严格限制。现有的高性能专家策略依赖昂贵的决策周期和大规模模型，无法直接部署于边缘设备或嵌入式平台。同时，现有的MARL知识蒸馏方法主要关注动作模仿，忽视了协调结构，且假设智能体能力均一，难以应对实际场景。

Method: 提出两阶段的知识蒸馏框架（KD-MARL），将协调行为从集中式专家迁移到轻量级去中心化学徒智能体。该方法无需评论家网络，依赖蒸馏的优势信号和结构化策略监督来保持协调性。支持异构学徒架构，使每个智能体的模型容量与其观测复杂度相匹配，适应部分或有限可观测性及资源受限环境。

Result: 在SMAC和MPE基准测试上的大量实验表明，KD-MARL在大幅降低计算成本的同时实现了高性能保持。在标准多智能体基准测试中，该方法保留了超过90%的专家性能，同时将计算成本（FLOPs）降低了高达28.6倍。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了专家级协调并通过结构化蒸馏予以保持，使MARL能够实际部署于资源受限的机载平台，为解决MARL实际应用中的资源瓶颈提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Real world deployment of multi agent reinforcement learning MARL systems is fundamentally constrained by limited compute memory and inference time. While expert policies achieve high performance they rely on costly decision cycles and large scale models that are impractical for edge devices or embedded platforms. Knowledge distillation KD offers a promising path toward resource aware execution but existing KD methods in MARL focus narrowly on action imitation often neglecting coordination structure and assuming uniform agent capabilities. We propose resource aware Knowledge Distillation for Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning KD MARL a two stage framework that transfers coordinated behavior from a centralized expert to lightweight decentralized student agents. The student policies are trained without a critic relying instead on distilled advantage signals and structured policy supervision to preserve coordination under heterogeneous and limited observations. Our approach transfers both action level behavior and structural coordination patterns from expert policies while supporting heterogeneous student architectures allowing each agent model capacity to match its observation complexity which is crucial for efficient execution under partial or limited observability and limited onboard resources. Extensive experiments on SMAC and MPE benchmarks demonstrate that KD MARL achieves high performance retention while substantially reducing computational cost. Across standard multi agent benchmarks KD MARL retains over 90 percent of expert performance while reducing computational cost by up to 28.6 times FLOPs. The proposed approach achieves expert level coordination and preserves it through structured distillation enabling practical MARL deployment across resource constrained onboard platforms.

</details>


### [14] [Reasoning Fails Where Step Flow Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06695)
*Xiaoyu Xu,Yulan Pan,Xiaosong Yuan,Zhihong Shen,Minghao Su,Yuanhao Su,Xiaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Step-Saliency诊断工具和StepFlow干预方法，揭示了大型推理模型中存在的Shallow Lock-in和Deep Decay两种信息流失败模式，并通过测试时干预提升了模型在多步推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在多步骤任务中表现良好但行为不稳定且难以解释，现有分析工具难以处理长且结构化的推理轨迹，需要新的方法来理解和修复其信息流问题。

Method: 1. 提出Step-Saliency方法：将注意力-梯度分数汇聚为沿问题-思考-摘要轨迹的步骤间映射图；2. 基于发现的两种信息流失败模式（Shallow Lock-in和Deep Decay），提出StepFlow干预方法：通过Odds-Equal Bridge调整浅层显著性模式，通过Step Momentum Injection在深层添加步骤级残差。

Result: Step-Saliency揭示了两种重复出现的信息流失败模式：浅层锁定（浅层过度关注当前步骤，几乎不使用早期上下文）和深层衰减（深层逐渐失去对思考段的显著性，摘要越来越关注自身和最后几步）；StepFlow在无需重新训练的情况下，提升了多个大型推理模型在数学、科学和编码任务上的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过修复信息流可以恢复大型推理模型缺失的部分推理性能，证明了信息流修复在提升模型推理能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) that generate long chains of thought now perform well on multi-step math, science, and coding tasks. However, their behavior is still unstable and hard to interpret, and existing analysis tools struggle with such long, structured reasoning traces. We introduce Step-Saliency, which pools attention--gradient scores into step-to-step maps along the question--thinking--summary trajectory. Across several models, Step-Saliency reveals two recurring information-flow failures: Shallow Lock-in, where shallow layers over-focus on the current step and barely use earlier context, and Deep Decay, where deep layers gradually lose saliency on the thinking segment and the summary increasingly attends to itself and the last few steps. Motivated by these patterns, we propose StepFlow, a saliency-inspired test-time intervention that adjusts shallow saliency patterns measured by Step-Saliency via Odds-Equal Bridge and adds a small step-level residual in deep layers via Step Momentum Injection. StepFlow improves accuracy on math, science, and coding tasks across multiple LRMs without retraining, indicating that repairing information flow can recover part of their missing reasoning performance.

</details>


### [15] [AgentGate: A Lightweight Structured Routing Engine for the Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06696)
*Yujun Cheng,Enfang Cui,Hao Qin,Zhiyuan Liang,Qi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentGate，一个面向AI代理系统的轻量级结构化路由引擎，将路由问题分解为动作决策和结构化落地两个阶段，并设计了面向路由的微调方案。实验表明，紧凑模型在受限场景下可实现有竞争力的路由性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理系统快速发展，正在形成"代理互联网"，但如何在延迟、隐私和成本约束下实现高效的请求分发仍是一个开放问题。现有工作虽改进了代理命名、发现和交互，但高效路由分发仍未解决。

Method: 提出AgentGate路由引擎，将路由问题形式化为约束决策问题，分解为两阶段：(1)动作决策阶段，判断查询应触发单代理调用、多代理规划、直接响应或安全升级；(2)结构化落地阶段，将选定动作实例化为可执行输出（目标代理、结构化参数或多步计划）。进一步开发了面向路由的微调方案，包含候选感知监督和困难负样本。

Result: 在精选路由基准上对多个3B-7B开源模型进行实验，结果表明紧凑模型在受限设置下可提供有竞争力的路由性能，模型差异主要体现在动作预测、候选选择和结构化落地质量上。

Conclusion: 结构化路由是构建高效且隐私感知的代理系统的可行设计点，尤其适用于资源受限部署条件下需要做出路由决策的场景。

Abstract: The rapid development of AI agent systems is leading to an emerging Internet of Agents, where specialized agents operate across local devices, edge nodes, private services, and cloud platforms. Although recent efforts have improved agent naming, discovery, and interaction, efficient request dispatch remains an open systems problem under latency, privacy, and cost constraints. In this paper, we present AgentGate, a lightweight structured routing engine for candidate-aware agent dispatch. Instead of treating routing as unrestricted text generation, AgentGate formulates it as a constrained decision problem and decomposes it into two stages: action decision and structural grounding. The first stage determines whether a query should trigger single-agent invocation, multi-agent planning, direct response, or safe escalation, while the second stage instantiates the selected action into executable outputs such as target agents, structured arguments, or multi-step plans. To adapt compact models to this setting, we further develop a routing-oriented fine-tuning scheme with candidate-aware supervision and hard negative examples. Experiments on a curated routing benchmark with several 3B--7B open-weight models show that compact models can provide competitive routing performance in constrained settings, and that model differences are mainly reflected in action prediction, candidate selection, and structured grounding quality. These results indicate that structured routing is a feasible design point for efficient and privacy-aware agent systems, especially when routing decisions must be made under resource-constrained deployment conditions.

</details>


### [16] [ATANT: An Evaluation Framework for AI Continuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06710)
*Samuel Sameer Tanguturi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ATANT，一个用于评估AI系统连续性能力的开放框架，包含7个必需属性、10个检查点的评估方法和250个故事的测试语料库，参考实现在大规模测试中达到96%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI行业虽然开发了多种记忆组件（RAG管道、向量数据库、长上下文窗口、配置文件层等），但缺乏正式定义和测量这些组件是否产生真正连续性的框架。需要一种系统化的方法来评估AI系统在跨时间维度上保持、更新、消歧和重建有意义上下文的能力。

Method: 定义连续性为具有7个必需属性的系统属性，引入无需LLM参与评估循环的10个检查点评估方法，构建包含250个故事、跨6个生活领域的1835个验证问题的叙事测试语料库。框架具有系统无关性、模型独立性和序列化方法论特点。

Result: 在5次测试套件迭代中评估参考实现：从传统架构的58%提升到隔离模式下的100%（250个故事），50故事累积模式下达到100%，250故事累积规模下达到96%。累积结果是主要衡量指标：当250个不同的生活叙事共存于同一数据库时，系统必须在正确上下文中检索正确事实而不发生交叉污染。

Conclusion: ATANT提供了一个系统无关、模型独立的连续性系统构建和验证的序列化方法论。框架规范、示例故事和评估协议已在GitHub开源，完整的250故事语料库将逐步发布，为AI系统的连续性能力提供了标准化评估基准。

Abstract: We present ATANT (Automated Test for Acceptance of Narrative Truth), an open evaluation framework for measuring continuity in AI systems: the ability to persist, update, disambiguate, and reconstruct meaningful context across time. While the AI industry has produced memory components (RAG pipelines, vector databases, long context windows, profile layers), no published framework formally defines or measures whether these components produce genuine continuity. We define continuity as a system property with 7 required properties, introduce a 10-checkpoint evaluation methodology that operates without an LLM in the evaluation loop, and present a narrative test corpus of 250 stories comprising 1,835 verification questions across 6 life domains. We evaluate a reference implementation across 5 test suite iterations, progressing from 58% (legacy architecture) to 100% in isolated mode (250 stories) and 100% in 50-story cumulative mode, with 96% at 250-story cumulative scale. The cumulative result is the primary measure: when 250 distinct life narratives coexist in the same database, the system must retrieve the correct fact for the correct context without cross-contamination. ATANT is system-agnostic, model-independent, and designed as a sequenced methodology for building and validating continuity systems. The framework specification, example stories, and evaluation protocol are available at https://github.com/Kenotic-Labs/ATANT. The full 250-story corpus will be released incrementally.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多模态大语言模型幻觉的可验证性控制方法，通过区分“明显幻觉”和“难以察觉幻觉”，在激活空间学习不同的探测探针，实现了对不同类型幻觉的精细化和灵活控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究忽略了多模态AI幻觉在可验证性上的差异——有些幻觉容易被人类检测（明显幻觉），有些则难以发现（难以察觉幻觉）。不同应用场景对安全性和可用性有不同要求，需要针对幻觉的可验证性进行精细化控制，但相关研究匮乏。

Method: 基于4,470个人类对AI生成幻觉的响应数据构建数据集，根据人类用户的可验证性将幻觉分为明显和难以察觉两类。提出一种激活空间干预方法，分别为两种幻觉类型学习独立的探测探针，并可通过简单混合实现灵活的可验证性控制。

Result: 明显幻觉和难以察觉幻觉激发出不同的干预探针，验证了两者的区分性。针对性干预在调节相应类型的可验证性方面表现优越，混合干预策略能够灵活适应不同场景下对可验证性的需求。

Conclusion: 本研究首次实现了对多模态大语言模型幻觉可验证性的精细化控制，为不同安全性和可用性需求的AI应用提供了有效的干预手段，显著提升了模型在实际应用中的可控性。

Abstract: AI applications driven by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are prone to hallucinations and pose considerable risks to human users. Crucially, such hallucinations are not equally problematic: some hallucination contents could be detected by human users(i.e., obvious hallucinations), while others are often missed or require more verification effort(i.e., elusive hallucinations). This indicates that multimodal AI hallucinations vary significantly in their verifiability. Yet, little research has explored how to control this property for AI applications with diverse security and usability demands. To address this gap, we construct a dataset from 4,470 human responses to AI-generated hallucinations and categorize these hallucinations into obvious and elusive types based on their verifiability by human users. Further, we propose an activation-space intervention method that learns separate probes for obvious and elusive hallucinations. We reveal that obvious and elusive hallucinations elicit different intervention probes, allowing for fine-grained control over the model's verifiability. Empirical results demonstrate the efficacy of this approach and show that targeted interventions yield superior performance in regulating corresponding verifiability. Moreover, simply mixing these interventions enables flexible control over the verifiability required for different scenarios.

</details>

*Juan Du,Yueteng Wu,Pan Zhao,Yuze Liu,Min Zhang,Xiaobin Xu,Xinglong Zhang*

TL;DR: 本文提出了TurboAgent，一个由大语言模型驱动的自主多智能体框架，实现了从自然语言需求到最终设计生成的跨声速单转子压缩机闭环气动设计与优化，在约30分钟内完成设计，关键性能指标R²超过0.91，并显著提升了等熵效率和总压比。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出TurboAgent框架，以大语言模型（LLM）为核心进行任务规划与协调，集成多个专用智能体分别负责生成式设计、快速性能预测、多目标优化和基于物理的验证。该框架将传统的试错设计转变为数据驱动的协同工作流程，并将高保真仿真保留用于最终验证。通过跨声速单转子压缩机进行验证。

Result: 目标性能、生成设计与CFD仿真之间高度一致。质量流量、总压比和等熵效率的决定系数（R²）均超过0.91，归一化均方根误差（RMSE）低于8%。优化智能体将等熵效率提升1.61%，总压比提升3.02%。在并行计算下，完整工作流程可在约30分钟内执行完成。

Conclusion: TurboAgent实现了从自然语言需求到最终设计生成的自主闭环设计过程，为涡轮机械气动设计提供了一个高效且可扩展的范式。

Abstract: The aerodynamic design of turbomachinery is a complex and tightly coupled multi-stage process involving geometry generation, performance prediction, optimization, and high-fidelity physical validation. Existing intelligent design approaches typically focus on individual stages or rely on loosely coupled pipelines, making fully autonomous end-to-end design challenging.To address this issue, this study proposes TurboAgent, a large language model (LLM)-driven autonomous multi-agent framework for turbomachinery aerodynamic design and optimization. The LLM serves as the core for task planning and coordination, while specialized agents handle generative design, rapid performance prediction, multi-objective optimization, and physics-based validation. The framework transforms traditional trial-and-error design into a data-driven collaborative workflow, with high-fidelity simulations retained for final verification.A transonic single-rotor compressor is used for validation. The results show strong agreement between target performance, generated designs, and CFD simulations. The coefficients of determination (R2) for mass flow rate, total pressure ratio, and isentropic efficiency all exceed 0.91, with normalized RMSE values below 8%. The optimization agent further improves isentropic efficiency by 1.61% and total pressure ratio by 3.02%. The complete workflow can be executed within approximately 30 minutes under parallel computing.
  These results demonstrate that TurboAgent enables an autonomous closed-loop design process from natural language requirements to final design generation, providing an efficient and scalable paradigm for turbomachinery aerodynamic design

</details>


### [19] [FVD: Inference-Time Alignment of Diffusion Models via Fleming-Viot Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06779)
*Shivanshu Shekhar,Sagnik Mukherjee,Jia Yi Zhang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Fleming-Viot Diffusion (FVD)，一种推理时对齐方法，通过用特殊的出生-死亡机制替代多项式重采样，解决了SMC扩散采样器中的多样性崩溃问题，在图像生成任务上显著提升性能且大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）的扩散采样器依赖多项式重采样或相关方案，在强选择压力下仍会导致多样性和谱系崩溃，限制了采样多样性和效果。

Method: 受Fleming-Viot种群动力学启发，FVD用专为扩散对齐设计的特殊出生-死亡机制替代多项式重采样。针对奖励仅近似可用且朴素重生会破坏确定性轨迹的情况，FVD将独立的基于奖励的生存决策与随机重生噪声相结合，形成灵活的种群动力学，无需价值函数近似或昂贵的推演。

Result: 在DrawBench上，ImageReward指标比先前方法提升7%；在类别条件生成任务上，FID相比强基线提升约14-20%，且速度比基于价值的方法快高达66倍。

Conclusion: FVD是一种完全可并行化、高效扩展推理计算的推理时对齐方法，能够在保持更广轨迹支持的同时有效探索奖励倾斜分布，在多种设置下取得显著性能提升，为扩散模型的对齐提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Fleming-Viot Diffusion (FVD), an inference-time alignment method that resolves the diversity collapse commonly observed in Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) based diffusion samplers. Existing SMC-based diffusion samplers often rely on multinomial resampling or closely related resampling schemes, which can still reduce diversity and lead to lineage collapse under strong selection pressure. Inspired by Fleming-Viot population dynamics, FVD replaces multinomial resampling with a specialized birth-death mechanism designed for diffusion alignment. To handle cases where rewards are only approximately available and naive rebirth would collapse deterministic trajectories, FVD integrates independent reward-based survival decisions with stochastic rebirth noise. This yields flexible population dynamics that preserve broader trajectory support while effectively exploring reward-tilted distributions, all without requiring value function approximation or costly rollouts. FVD is fully parallelizable and scales efficiently with inference compute. Empirically, it achieves substantial gains across settings: on DrawBench it outperforms prior methods by 7% in ImageReward, while on class-conditional tasks it improves FID by roughly 14-20% over strong baselines and is up to 66 times faster than value-based approaches.

</details>


### [20] [Riemann-Bench: A Benchmark for Moonshot Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06802)
*Suhaas Garre,Erik Knutsen,Sushant Mehta,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为\\bench{}的私密基准测试，包含25道由专家精心设计的数学问题，旨在评估前沿AI系统在研究级数学上的能力。结果显示，所有前沿模型在这些问题上的得分均低于10%，揭示了竞赛级数学与研究级数学之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在国际数学奥林匹克（IMO）等竞赛中表现出色，但竞赛数学仅代表数学推理的狭窄领域，问题来源有限、所需高级理论较少，且常依赖巧妙技巧而非深厚理论知识。为了更真实地评估AI在研究级数学上的能力，作者设计了这一基准。

Method: 构建包含25道专家（常春藤盟校教授、研究生及IMO奖牌获得者）原创问题的私密基准。每题需两位独立领域专家盲审并从头求解，答案为唯一闭式解，由程序验证。评估时，前沿模型作为无约束研究代理，拥有完整编程工具、搜索与开放推理权限，每题独立运行100次，采用无偏统计估计器计算得分。

Result: 所有前沿模型在该基准上的得分均低于10%，表明当前AI在研究级数学推理能力上远逊于其在竞赛级数学上的表现。由于基准完全私密，结果反映了模型的真实数学能力，而非对训练数据的记忆。

Conclusion: 竞赛级数学与研究级数学之间存在显著鸿沟，当前前沿AI模型尚不具备真正的研究级数学推理能力。私密基准可有效防止数据泄露，确保评估结果的公正性与真实性。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have achieved gold-medal-level performance on the International Mathematical Olympiad, demonstrating remarkable proficiency at competition-style problem solving. However, competition mathematics represents only a narrow slice of mathematical reasoning: problems are drawn from limited domains, require minimal advanced machinery, and can often reward insightful tricks over deep theoretical knowledge. We introduce \bench{}, a private benchmark of 25 expert-curated problems designed to evaluate AI systems on research-level mathematics that goes far beyond the olympiad frontier. Problems are authored by Ivy League mathematics professors, graduate students, and PhD-holding IMO medalists, and routinely took their authors weeks to solve independently. Each problem undergoes double-blind verification by two independent domain experts who must solve the problem from scratch, and yields a unique, closed-form solution assessed by programmatic verifiers. We evaluate frontier models as unconstrained research agents, with full access to coding tools, search, and open-ended reasoning, using an unbiased statistical estimator computed over 100 independent runs per problem. Our results reveal that all frontier models currently score below 10\%, exposing a substantial gap between olympiad-level problem solving and genuine research-level mathematical reasoning. By keeping the benchmark fully private, we ensure that measured performance reflects authentic mathematical capability rather than memorization of training data.

</details>


### [21] [Beyond Surface Judgments: Human-Grounded Risk Evaluation of LLM-Generated Disinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06820)
*Zonghuan Xu,Xiang Zheng,Yutao Wu,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了LLM评判器在评估读者反应方面的有效性，发现LLM评判器与人类读者之间存在持续性差距：评判器更严苛、对条目排序恢复能力弱、依赖不同的文本信号，且评判器内部一致性远高于与人类的一致性，表明内部一致性不能作为替代读者反应有效性的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够大规模生成具有说服力的叙事，引发了对其在虚假信息活动中潜在用途的担忧。评估这一风险需要理解读者如何接收此类内容。然而在实践中，LLM评判器被越来越多地用作直接人类评估的低成本替代品，但它们是否能忠实地追踪读者反应仍不清楚。

Method: 将此场景下的评估重新定义为代理有效性问题，使用290篇对齐文章、2,043对人类评分以及八个前沿评判器的输出，从整体评分、条目级排序和信号依赖性三个方面审计LLM评判器与人类读者反应的对齐程度。

Result: 发现评判器与人类之间存在持续差距：相对于人类，评判器通常更严苛，仅能微弱地恢复条目级的人类排名，并依赖不同的文本信号，更注重逻辑严谨性，同时对情感强度的惩罚更强烈。同时，评判器之间的相互一致性远高于与人类读者的一致性。

Conclusion: LLM评判器形成了一个连贯的评估群体，其内部一致性远高于与人类读者的一致性，这表明内部一致性并不能作为替代读者反应有效性的证据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate persuasive narratives at scale, raising concerns about their potential use in disinformation campaigns. Assessing this risk ultimately requires understanding how readers receive such content. In practice, however, LLM judges are increasingly used as a low-cost substitute for direct human evaluation, even though whether they faithfully track reader responses remains unclear. We recast evaluation in this setting as a proxy-validity problem and audit LLM judges against human reader responses. Using 290 aligned articles, 2,043 paired human ratings, and outputs from eight frontier judges, we examine judge--human alignment in terms of overall scoring, item-level ordering, and signal dependence. We find persistent judge--human gaps throughout. Relative to humans, judges are typically harsher, recover item-level human rankings only weakly, and rely on different textual signals, placing more weight on logical rigour while penalizing emotional intensity more strongly. At the same time, judges agree far more with one another than with human readers. These results suggest that LLM judges form a coherent evaluative group that is much more aligned internally than it is with human readers, indicating that internal agreement is not evidence of validity as a proxy for reader response.

</details>


### [22] [Explaining Neural Networks in Preference Learning: a Post-hoc Inductive Logic Programming Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06838)
*Daniele Fossemò,Filippo Mignosi,Giuseppe Placidi,Luca Raggioli,Matteo Spezialetti,Fabio Aurelio D'Asaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用ILASP系统（基于回答集程序的归纳学习）通过弱约束来近似神经网络模型，在用户偏好学习场景中实现黑盒模型的可解释性。结合PCA降维技术，在保持解释透明度的同时解决了高维特征空间的计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络等黑盒模型缺乏可解释性，特别是在用户偏好学习场景中，用户需要理解模型为何做出特定偏好预测。现有方法难以在高维特征空间中既保持模型保真度又提供透明的解释。

Method: 1. 创建食谱偏好数据集用于训练神经网络；
2. 使用ILASP系统通过弱约束学习回答集程序来近似神经网络；
3. 分别探索ILASP作为全局近似器和局部近似器；
4. 引入PCA预处理步骤降低数据维度，同时保持解释的透明度。

Result: 实验验证了ILASP能够有效近似神经网络模型，在逐步增高的特征维度空间中实现了目标模型的适当保真度，同时限制了计算时间的增长。PCA预处理成功降低了维度并保持了透明性。

Conclusion: 本研究表明基于回答集程序的学习方法可以有效地近似黑盒神经网络模型，为偏好学习系统提供了可解释的替代方案。结合PCA降维技术，该方法能够在高维场景中平衡保真度、计算效率和透明度。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose using Learning from Answer Sets to approximate black-box models, such as Neural Networks (NN), in the specific case of learning user preferences. We specifically explore the use of ILASP (Inductive Learning of Answer Set Programs) to approximate preference learning systems through weak constraints. We have created a dataset on user preferences over a set of recipes, which is used to train the NNs that we aim to approximate with ILASP. Our experiments investigate ILASP both as a global and a local approximator of the NNs. These experiments address the challenge of approximating NNs working on increasingly high-dimensional feature spaces while achieving appropriate fidelity on the target model and limiting the increase in computational time. To handle this challenge, we propose a preprocessing step that exploits Principal Component Analysis to reduce the dataset's dimensionality while keeping our explanations transparent. Under consideration for publication in Theory and Practice of Logic Programming (TPLP).

</details>


### [23] [What's Missing in Screen-to-Action? Towards a UI-in-the-Loop Paradigm for Multimodal GUI Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06995)
*Songze Li,Xiaoke Guo,Tianqi Liu,Biao Yi,Zhaoyan Gong,Zhiqiang Liu,Huajun Chen,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出UILoop框架，通过“屏幕-UI元素-动作”循环范式增强GUI推理的可解释性和UI理解能力，并构建了包含26K样本的UI理解基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI推理方法主要依赖直接基于屏幕的决策，缺乏可解释性且忽视对UI元素的全面理解，导致任务失败。

Method: 提出UI-in-the-Loop (UILoop)范式，将GUI推理建模为循环的“屏幕-UI元素-动作”过程，使多模态大语言模型显式学习关键UI元素的定位、语义功能和实际使用；同时提出UI理解任务及三种评估指标，构建26K样本的基准测试UI Comprehension-Bench。

Result: UILoop在UI理解任务上达到最先进性能，同时在GUI推理任务中取得优异结果。

Conclusion: UILoop通过显式建模UI元素的理解过程，有效提升了GUI推理的可解释性和任务成功率，为UI理解和交互提供了新范式和评测基准。

Abstract: Existing Graphical User Interface (GUI) reasoning tasks remain challenging, particularly in UI understanding. Current methods typically rely on direct screen-based decision-making, which lacks interpretability and overlooks a comprehensive understanding of UI elements, ultimately leading to task failure. To enhance the understanding and interaction with UIs, we propose an innovative GUI reasoning paradigm called UI-in-the-Loop (UILoop). Our approach treats the GUI reasoning task as a cyclic Screen-UI elements-Action process. By enabling Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to explicitly learn the localization, semantic functions, and practical usage of key UI elements, UILoop achieves precise element discovery and performs interpretable reasoning. Furthermore, we introduce a more challenging UI Comprehension task centered on UI elements with three evaluation metrics. Correspondingly, we contribute a benchmark of 26K samples (UI Comprehension-Bench) to comprehensively evaluate existing methods' mastery of UI elements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UILoop achieves state-of-the-art UI understanding performance while yielding superior results in GUI reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [24] [EmoMAS: Emotion-Aware Multi-Agent System for High-Stakes Edge-Deployable Negotiation with Bayesian Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07003)
*Yunbo Long,Yunhan Liu,Liming Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了EmoMAS，一个贝叶斯多智能体框架，使小语言模型（SLM）能够在边缘设备上进行高性能、保护隐私的高风险谈判，通过将情绪表达视为战略变量，显著超越了基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在自动化谈判中计算成本高且存在隐私风险，难以部署在移动助手或救援机器人等隐私敏感的边缘设备上；而小语言模型（SLM）虽具备部署优势，却难以应对高风险谈判中复杂的情绪动态。

Method: 引入EmoMAS框架，利用贝叶斯协调器统筹博弈论、强化学习和心理连贯性三个专业智能体；通过融合实时洞察来优化情绪状态转换，并根据谈判反馈持续更新智能体可靠性，构建了无需预训练的在线策略学习混合智能体架构。

Result: 在债务、医疗、应急响应和教育四个新提出的高风险谈判基准测试中，配备EmoMAS的SLM和LLM在谈判性能上始终超越所有基线模型，并能有效平衡伦理行为。

Conclusion: 策略性情绪智能是谈判成功的关键驱动力；通过将情绪表达作为贝叶斯多智能体优化框架内的战略变量，EmoMAS为高风险边缘部署建立了一种有效、隐私保护且自适应的谈判AI新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) has been widely used for automated negotiation, but their high computational cost and privacy risks limit deployment in privacy-sensitive, on-device settings such as mobile assistants or rescue robots. Small language models (SLMs) offer a viable alternative, yet struggle with the complex emotional dynamics of high-stakes negotiation. We introduces EmoMAS, a Bayesian multi-agent framework that transforms emotional decision-making from reactive to strategic. EmoMAS leverages a Bayesian orchestrator to coordinate three specialized agents: game-theoretic, reinforcement learning, and psychological coherence models. The system fuses their real-time insights to optimize emotional state transitions while continuously updating agent reliability based on negotiation feedback. This mixture-of-agents architecture enables online strategy learning without pre-training. We further introduce four high-stakes, edge-deployable negotiation benchmarks across debt, healthcare, emergency response, and educational domains. Through extensive agent-to-agent simulations across all benchmarks, both SLMs and LLMs equipped with EmoMAS consistently surpass all baseline models in negotiation performance while balancing ethical behavior. These results show that strategic emotional intelligence is also the key driver of negotiation success. By treating emotional expression as a strategic variable within a Bayesian multi-agent optimization framework, EmoMAS establishes a new paradigm for effective, private, and adaptive negotiation AI suitable for high-stakes edge deployment.

</details>


### [25] [CAFP: A Post-Processing Framework for Group Fairness via Counterfactual Model Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07009)
*Irina Arévalo,Marcos Oliva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAFP，一种模型无关的后处理公平性方法，通过平均事实和反事实预测来消除敏感属性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性干预方法通常需要完全控制模型架构和访问受保护属性信息，在实际系统中往往不可行。

Method: CAFP通过生成敏感属性翻转的反事实样本，对原始模型在事实和反事实实例上的预测进行平均，无需重新训练或修改原始分类器。

Result: 理论证明CAFP消除了对受保护属性的直接依赖，降低了预测与敏感属性间的互信息，在温和假设下实现完美的人口统计均等，并将机会均等差距至少减少一半的平均反事实偏差。

Conclusion: CAFP作为一种模型无关的后处理方法，在不修改原始模型的前提下有效缓解了公平性问题，具有理论保障和实际可行性。

Abstract: Ensuring fairness in machine learning predictions is a critical challenge, especially when models are deployed in sensitive domains such as credit scoring, healthcare, and criminal justice. While many fairness interventions rely on data preprocessing or algorithmic constraints during training, these approaches often require full control over the model architecture and access to protected attribute information, which may not be feasible in real-world systems. In this paper, we propose Counterfactual Averaging for Fair Predictions (CAFP), a model-agnostic post-processing method that mitigates unfair influence from protected attributes without retraining or modifying the original classifier. CAFP operates by generating counterfactual versions of each input in which the sensitive attribute is flipped, and then averaging the model's predictions across factual and counterfactual instances. We provide a theoretical analysis of CAFP, showing that it eliminates direct dependence on the protected attribute, reduces mutual information between predictions and sensitive attributes, and provably bounds the distortion introduced relative to the original model. Under mild assumptions, we further show that CAFP achieves perfect demographic parity and reduces the equalized odds gap by at least half the average counterfactual bias.

</details>


### [26] [A-MBER: Affective Memory Benchmark for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07017)
*Deliang Wen,Ke Sun,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了A-MBER，一个用于评估AI助手基于记忆历史进行情感识别能力的新基准，填补了现有数据集在长期情感记忆推理方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的情感数据集主要评估瞬时情感，而长期记忆基准主要关注事实回忆和一致性，缺乏对模型利用历史交互记忆来理解用户当前情感状态能力的评估资源。

Method: 构建A-MBER基准，采用分阶段流水线（包括长期规划、对话生成、标注、问题构建和最终打包），支持判断、检索和解释任务，以及模态降级和证据不足等鲁棒性设置。模型需要基于多轮交互历史推断当前情感状态、识别历史相关证据并提供依据。

Result: 实验对比了局部上下文、长上下文、检索记忆、结构化记忆和黄金证据等条件，发现A-MBER在长程隐式情感、高依赖记忆层级、轨迹推理和对抗设置等子集上具有显著区分能力。

Conclusion: 记忆对情感解释的支持不仅仅是提供更多历史信息，更重要的是使模型能够更选择性、有依据和情境敏感地利用过去的交互信息。

Abstract: AI assistants that interact with users over time need to interpret the user's current emotional state in order to respond appropriately and personally. However, this capability remains insufficiently evaluated. Existing emotion datasets mainly assess local or instantaneous affect, while long-term memory benchmarks focus largely on factual recall, temporal consistency, or knowledge updating. As a result, current resources provide limited support for testing whether a model can use remembered interaction history to interpret a user's present affective state.
  We introduce A-MBER, an Affective Memory Benchmark for Emotion Recognition, to evaluate this capability. A-MBER focuses on present affective interpretation grounded in remembered multi-session interaction history. Given an interaction trajectory and a designated anchor turn, a model must infer the user's current affective state, identify historically relevant evidence, and justify its interpretation in a grounded way. The benchmark is constructed through a staged pipeline with explicit intermediate representations, including long-horizon planning, conversation generation, annotation, question construction, and final packaging. It supports judgment, retrieval, and explanation tasks, together with robustness settings such as modality degradation and insufficient-evidence conditions.
  Experiments compare local-context, long-context, retrieved-memory, structured-memory, and gold-evidence conditions within a unified framework. Results show that A-MBER is especially discriminative on the subsets it is designed to stress, including long-range implicit affect, high-dependency memory levels, trajectory-based reasoning, and adversarial settings. These findings suggest that memory supports affective interpretation not simply by providing more history, but by enabling more selective, grounded, and context-sensitive use of past interaction

</details>


### [27] [Planning Task Shielding: Detecting and Repairing Flaws in Planning Tasks through Turning them Unsolvable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07042)
*Alberto Pozanco,Marianela Morales,Pietro Totis,Daniel Borrajo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了规划任务屏蔽的概念，旨在检测并修复规划任务中的缺陷，通过最优算法$allmin$最小化修改原始动作，使规划任务不可解，从而确保系统永远不会到达缺陷状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的规划研究侧重于生成实现目标的计划，但在某些场景下，目标可以编码一个“永远不应成立”的性质（即缺陷状态）。此时研究目标转变为：如何修改规划任务，使其永远无法到达缺陷状态，即让规划任务变得不可解。这种需求催生了规划任务屏蔽问题。

Method: 提出了规划任务屏蔽问题的形式化定义，并设计了最优算法$allmin$。该算法通过最小化修改原始动作集，使规划任务变得不可解，从而确保任何可能的规划轨迹都无法到达缺陷状态。

Result: 在规模递增的规划任务上对$allmin$算法进行了实证评估。实验结果表明，该算法能够有效地对系统进行屏蔽，通过将规划任务转变为不可解状态来防止缺陷状态的出现。

Conclusion: 规划任务屏蔽是规划和验证领域的一个新研究方向，$allmin$算法提供了一种最优且实用的解决方案，能够以最小的修改代价确保系统的安全性。

Abstract: Most research in planning focuses on generating a plan to achieve a desired set of goals. However, a goal specification can also be used to encode a property that should never hold, allowing a planner to identify a trace that would reach a flawed state. In such cases, the objective may shift to modifying the planning task to ensure that the flawed state is never reached-in other words, to make the planning task unsolvable. In this paper we introduce planning task shielding: the problem of detecting and repairing flaws in planning tasks. We propose $allmin$, an optimal algorithm that solves these tasks by minimally modifying the original actions to render the planning task unsolvable. We empirically evaluate the performance of $allmin$ in shielding planning tasks of increasing size, showing how it can effectively shield the system by turning the planning task unsolvable.

</details>


### [28] [EVGeoQA: Benchmarking LLMs on Dynamic, Multi-Objective Geo-Spatial Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07070)
*Jianfei Wu,Zhichun Wang,Zhensheng Wang,Zhiyu He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了EVGeoQA基准和GeoRover评估框架，用于测试大语言模型在动态地理空间环境中进行多目标探索的能力，发现模型在长距离空间探索上存在困难，但具备总结历史轨迹以提升效率的新兴能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间问答基准主要关注静态检索，未能捕捉真实世界规划中涉及动态用户位置和复合约束的复杂性，缺乏对大语言模型在动态地理空间环境中目的驱动探索能力的研究。

Method: 提出EVGeoQA基准，基于电动汽车充电场景，采用位置锚定和双目标设计（充电需求+共址活动偏好）；提出GeoRover评估框架，基于工具增强的智能体架构来评估大语言模型在动态、多目标探索中的能力。

Result: 实验表明：大语言模型能够成功利用工具解决子任务，但在长距离空间探索方面存在困难；同时观察到一个新兴能力——大语言模型能够总结历史探索轨迹以提高探索效率。

Conclusion: EVGeoQA为未来地理空间智能研究建立了一个具有挑战性的测试平台，揭示了大语言模型在动态空间探索中的优势与局限性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable reasoning capabilities, their potential for purpose-driven exploration in dynamic geo-spatial environments remains under-investigated. Existing Geo-Spatial Question Answering (GSQA) benchmarks predominantly focus on static retrieval, failing to capture the complexity of real-world planning that involves dynamic user locations and compound constraints. To bridge this gap, we introduce EVGeoQA, a novel benchmark built upon Electric Vehicle (EV) charging scenarios that features a distinct location-anchored and dual-objective design. Specifically, each query in EVGeoQA is explicitly bound to a user's real-time coordinate and integrates the dual objectives of a charging necessity and a co-located activity preference. To systematically assess models in such complex settings, we further propose GeoRover, a general evaluation framework based on a tool-augmented agent architecture to evaluate the LLMs' capacity for dynamic, multi-objective exploration. Our experiments reveal that while LLMs successfully utilize tools to address sub-tasks, they struggle with long-range spatial exploration. Notably, we observe an emergent capability: LLMs can summarize historical exploration trajectories to enhance exploration efficiency. These findings establish EVGeoQA as a challenging testbed for future geo-spatial intelligence. The dataset and prompts are available at https://github.com/Hapluckyy/EVGeoQA/.

</details>


### [29] [Reason in Chains, Learn in Trees: Self-Rectification and Grafting for Multi-turn Agent Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07165)
*Yu Li,Sizhe Tang,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出T-STAR框架，通过构建认知树恢复独立轨迹间的潜在奖励结构，解决大模型强化学习中多步推理任务的稀疏奖励问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法（如GRPO）将采样轨迹视为独立链条，对所有步骤分配统一信用，忽略了关键步骤对推理结果的非均衡影响，导致在多步推理任务中受稀疏奖励限制。

Method: 1. 将轨迹整合为统一的认知树，识别并合并功能相似的步骤/节点；2. 提出内省估值机制，通过树结构反向传播轨迹级奖励，获得方差降低的步骤级相对优势；3. 开发上下文思维嫁接技术，在关键分歧点对比成功与失败分支合成修正推理；4. 设计手术式策略优化，通过Bradley-Terry型损失聚焦关键点的策略梯度信息。

Result: 在具身、交互、推理和规划基准测试上的大量实验表明，T-STAR相比强基线方法取得了一致的性能提升，在需要长推理链的任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: T-STAR通过恢复潜在奖励结构、聚焦关键步骤优化，有效解决了大模型智能体在多步推理任务中的稀疏奖励问题，为复杂推理任务提供了一种新的强化学习范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for Large Language Model agents is often hindered by sparse rewards in multi-step reasoning tasks. Existing approaches like Group Relative Policy Optimization treat sampled trajectories as independent chains, assigning uniform credit to all steps in each chain and ignoring the existence of critical steps that may disproportionally impact reasoning outcome. In this paper, we propose T-STAR(Tree-structured Self-Taught Agent Rectification), a framework that recovers the latent correlated reward structure across seemingly independent trajectories. Specifically, we consolidate trajectories into a unified Cognitive Tree by identifying and merging functionally similar steps/nodes. It enables an Introspective Valuation mechanism that back-propagates trajectory-level rewards through the tree to obtain a new notion of variance-reduced relative advantage at step-level. Using the Cognitive Tree, we also develop In-Context Thought Grafting to synthesize corrective reasoning by contrasting successful and failed branches at critical divergence points/steps. Our proposed Surgical Policy Optimization then capitalizes on the rich policy gradient information concentrated at these critical points/steps through a Bradley-Terry type of surgical loss. Extensive experiments across embodied, interactive, reasoning, and planning benchmarks demonstrate that T-STAR achieves consistent improvements over strong baselines, with gains most pronounced on tasks requiring extended reasoning chains.

</details>


### [30] [How Much LLM Does a Self-Revising Agent Actually Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07236)
*Seongwoo Jeong,Seonil Son*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种声明式反射运行时协议，通过外部化智能体状态和反思机制，成功解耦了LLM能力与显式结构贡献，发现显式世界模型规划带来显著提升(+24.1pp胜率)，而稀疏LLM修订的作用有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体将世界建模、规划和反思集成在单一语言模型循环中，导致难以回答一个基本科学问题：智能体的能力究竟有多少来自LLM本身，又有多少来自其周围的显式结构？作者旨在通过实证方法使该问题变得可研究。

Method: 引入声明式反射运行时协议，将智能体状态、置信度信号、保护性动作和假设性转换外部化为可检查的运行时结构。在声明式运行时中实例化该协议，在噪声协作战舰游戏中评估四个渐进式结构化智能体，共进行54场游戏(18个棋盘×3个种子)。将系统分解为四个组件：后验信念追踪、显式世界模型规划、符号化回合内反思、以及稀疏LLM修订。

Result: 显式世界模型规划相比贪婪后验跟随基线显著提升(+24.1pp胜率，+0.017 F1)。符号化反思作为运行时机制有效运行(包含预测追踪、置信度门控和保护性修订动作)，但其当前修订预设总体上尚未带来净正向收益。在约4.3%的回合添加条件性LLM修订仅产生小幅且非单调的变化：平均F1略微上升(+0.005)，但胜率下降(54场中从31降至29)。

Conclusion: 这是一个方法论贡献而非排行榜声明：外部化反思将原本潜在的智能体行为转化为可检查的运行时结构，使得LLM干预的边际作用能够被直接研究，为理解LLM在智能体中的真实贡献提供了实证分析框架。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based agents often place world modeling, planning, and reflection inside a single language model loop. This can produce capable behavior, but it makes a basic scientific question difficult to answer: which part of the agent's competence actually comes from the LLM, and which part comes from explicit structure around it?
  We study this question not by claiming a general answer, but by making it empirically tractable. We introduce a declared reflective runtime protocol that externalizes agent state, confidence signals, guarded actions, and hypothetical transitions into inspectable runtime structure. We instantiate this protocol in a declarative runtime and evaluate it on noisy Collaborative Battleship [4] using four progressively structured agents over 54 games (18 boards $\times$ 3 seeds).
  The resulting decomposition isolates four components: posterior belief tracking, explicit world-model planning, symbolic in-episode reflection, and sparse LLM-based revision. Across this decomposition, explicit world-model planning improves substantially over a greedy posterior-following baseline (+24.1pp win rate, +0.017 F1). Symbolic reflection operates as a real runtime mechanism -- with prediction tracking, confidence gating, and guarded revision actions -- even though its current revision presets are not yet net-positive in aggregate. Adding conditional LLM revision at about 4.3\% of turns yields only a small and non-monotonic change: average F1 rises slightly (+0.005) while win rate drops (31$\rightarrow$29 out of 54).
  These results suggest a methodological contribution rather than a leaderboard claim: externalizing reflection turns otherwise latent agent behavior into inspectable runtime structure, allowing the marginal role of LLM intervention to be studied directly.

</details>

### [31] [CoverAssert: Iterative LLM Assertion Generation Driven by Functional Coverage via Syntax-Semantic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06607)
*Yonghao Wang,Yang Yin,Hongqin Lyu,Jiaxin Zhou,Zhiteng Chao,Mingyu Shi,Wenchao Ding,Yunlin Du,Jing Ye,Tiancheng Wang,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出CoverAssert框架，通过迭代方式和覆盖率反馈指导LLM生成SystemVerilog断言，显著提升了功能覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM单次生成SystemVerilog断言（SVA）的方法由于对集成电路设计理解有限，生成的断言往往功能覆盖率不足。

Method: 提出CoverAssert迭代框架：1) 聚合断言的语义和基于AST的结构特征进行聚类；2) 将聚类结果映射到规范；3) 利用功能覆盖率反馈指导LLM优先处理未覆盖的覆盖点。

Result: 在四个开源设计上的实验表明，将CoverAssert与AssertLLM和Spec2Assertion集成后，分支覆盖率平均提升9.57%，语句覆盖率提升9.64%，翻转覆盖率提升15.69%。

Conclusion: CoverAssert框架能有效弥补LLM在硬件设计理解上的不足，通过迭代反馈机制显著提升了自动生成断言的功能覆盖质量。

Abstract: LLMs can generate SystemVerilog assertions (SVAs) from natural language specs, but single-pass outputs often lack functional coverage due to limited IC design understanding. We propose CoverAssert, an iterative framework that clusters semantic and AST-based structural features of assertions, maps them to specifications, and uses functional coverage feedback to guide LLMs in prioritizing uncovered points. Experiments on four open-source designs show that integrating CoverAssert with AssertLLM and Spec2Assertion improves average improvements of 9.57 % in branch coverage, 9.64 % in statement coverage, and 15.69 % in toggle coverage.

</details>


### [32] [SwarmIO: Towards 100 Million IOPS SSD Emulation for Next-generation GPU-centric Storage Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06668)
*Hyeseong Kim,Gwangoo Yeo,Minsoo Rhu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了SwarmIO，一个面向大规模并行GPU中心存储系统的SSD模拟器，解决了现有模拟器在GPU发起I/O场景下的三大挑战，实现了比现有技术快303.9倍的模拟速度，并在向量搜索案例中展示了将SSD IOPS从2.5M提升至40M可带来最高9.7倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU发起的I/O和高IOPS优化SSD的结合推动了GPU中心存储系统的兴起，但现有框架无法对此类系统进行定量的端到端评估。传统SSD模拟器在面对GPU中心场景时存在三大挑战：处理海量请求流的前端扩展性受限、模拟GPU发起I/O控制和数据路径的软件开销高、在极高I/O请求率下时序模型维护开销过大。

Method: 提出SwarmIO SSD模拟器，专门针对大规模并行GPU中心存储进行设计。该模拟器针对前述三大挑战进行了优化：扩展前端处理能力以应对海量请求流、降低GPU发起I/O控制与数据路径模拟的软件开销、减少高I/O请求率下的时序模型维护开销。能够忠实建模目标性能高达40 MIOPS的IOPS优化SSD。

Result: SwarmIO在GPU发起I/O场景下相比现有最先进基准SSD模拟器实现了303.9倍的速度提升。通过向量搜索案例研究证明，将SSD IOPS从2.5 MIOPS提升至40 MIOPS，可获得平均最高9.7倍的端到端性能加速。

Conclusion: SwarmIO填补了GPU中心存储系统定量端到端评估工具的空白，为IOPS优化的GPU发起I/O存储系统研究提供了有效的实验平台，证明了高IOPS SSD在GPU加速应用中的显著性能潜力。

Abstract: GPU-initiated I/O has emerged as a key mechanism for achieving high-throughput storage access by leveraging massive GPU thread-level parallelism, while recent industry trends point toward SSDs optimized for ultra-high random-read IOPS. Together, these trends are enabling the emergence of IOPS-optimized, GPU-centric storage systems. Despite this momentum, no existing framework enables quantitative end-to-end evaluation of storage systems optimized for GPU-initiated I/O. While conventional SSD emulators provide a promising path toward end-to-end modeling in traditional storage systems, they face three key challenges in this GPU-centric setting: limited frontend scalability for ingesting massive request streams, high software overhead in emulating GPU-initiated I/O control and data paths, and excessive timing-model maintenance overhead at extremely high I/O request rates. We propose SwarmIO, an SSD emulator for massively parallel, GPU-centric storage. SwarmIO faithfully models IOPS-optimized SSDs at target performance levels of up to 40 MIOPS, achieving a 303.9x speedup over the state-of-the-art baseline SSD emulator under GPU-initiated I/O. We further demonstrate its utility through a vector search case study, showing that increasing SSD IOPS from 2.5 MIOPS to 40 MIOPS yields an average end-to-end speedup of up to 9.7x.

</details>

*Kanta Yoshioka,Soshi Hirayae,Yuichiro Tanaka,Yuichi Katori,Takashi Morie,Hakaru Tamukoh*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向自主边缘AI对实时决策和轻量级适应性的需求，同时解决现有数字混沌动力学处理器(CDP)面临的高计算成本和面积开销挑战。

Method: 采用大规模全连接1024神经元混沌玻尔兹曼机(CBM)架构；提出CBM特定调度器，利用固有低神经元翻转率减少乘累加操作；设计高效乘法拆分方案以降低硬件面积。

Result: 在65nm工艺(12mm²)成功流片；乘累加操作减少99%，面积降低59%；在SA和RC领域分别实现25-54倍和4.5倍的能效提升。

Conclusion: CBM-Dual实现了异构任务的同时执行，为自主边缘AI应用提供了高能效、实时的混沌动力学计算解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents CBM-Dual, the first silicon-proven digital chaotic dynamics processor (CDP) supporting both simulated annealing (SA) and reservoir computing (RC). CBM-Dual enables real-time decision-making and lightweight adaptation for autonomous Edge AI, employing the largest-scale fully connected 1024-neuron chaotic Boltzmann machine (CBM). To address the high computational and area costs of digital CDPs, we propose: 1) a CBM-specific scheduler that exploits an inherently low neuron flip rate to reduce multiply-accumulate operations by 99%, and 2) an efficient multiply splitting scheme that reduces the area by 59%. Fabricated in 65nm (12mm$^2$), CBM-Dual achieves simultaneous heterogeneous task execution and state-of-the-art energy efficiency, delivering $\times$25-54 and $\times$4.5 improvements in the SA and RC fields, respectively.

</details>


### [34] [TRAPTI: Time-Resolved Analysis for SRAM Banking and Power Gating Optimization in Embedded Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06955)
*Jan Klhufek,Alberto Marchisio,Vojtech Mrazek,Lukas Sekanina,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAPTI方法论，通过周期级仿真和片上内存占用分析，揭示GQA相比MHA在片上内存利用上的显著优势，峰值降低2.72倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer网络在嵌入式硬件部署受限于严格的面积、延迟和能耗约束。推理过程中，KV缓存的内存占用随序列长度增长，成为性能瓶颈。现有GQA机制虽能减少KV缓存需求，但缺乏对片上内存需求随时间演化的深入理解，难以有效利用这一优势。

Method: 提出TRAPTI两阶段方法论：第一阶段通过周期级推理仿真获取内存占用轨迹和访问统计；第二阶段利用这些轨迹离线优化存储体组织结构和电源门控配置。该方法应用于GPT-2 XL（MHA）和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B（GQA）的对比分析。

Result: 在相同加速器配置下，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B（GQA）相比GPT-2 XL（MHA）实现了峰值片上内存利用率2.72倍的降低，为电源门控优化提供了更多机会。

Conclusion: TRAPTI方法论能够有效指导Transformer加速器的片上内存设计优化。GQA架构相比传统MHA在片上内存利用方面具有显著优势，可实现更高的内存效率，从而降低嵌入式部署的硬件约束。

Abstract: Transformer neural networks achieve state-of-the-art accuracy across language and vision tasks, but their deployment on embedded hardware is hindered by stringent area, latency, and energy constraints. During inference, performance and efficiency are increasingly dominated by the Key--Value (KV) cache, whose memory footprint grows with sequence length, straining on-chip memory utilization. Although existing mechanisms such as Grouped-Query Attention (GQA) reduce KV cache requirements compared to Multi-Head Attention (MHA), effectively exploiting this reduction requires understanding how on-chip memory demand evolves over time. This work presents TRAPTI, a two-stage methodology that combines cycle-level inference simulation with time-resolved analysis of on-chip memory occupancy to guide design decisions. In the first stage, the framework obtains memory occupancy traces and memory access statistics from simulation. In the second stage, the framework leverages the traces to explore banked memory organizations and power-gating configurations in an offline optimization flow. We apply this methodology to GPT-2 XL and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B under the same accelerator configuration, enabling a direct comparison of MHA and GQA memory profiles. The analysis shows that DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B exhibits a 2.72x reduction in peak on-chip memory utilization in this setting compared to GPT-2 XL, unlocking further opportunities for power-gating optimization.

</details>

*Avinash Mahesh Nirmala,Dominik Walter,Frank Hannig,Jürgen Teich*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种符号化分析方法，用于在并行处理器阵列加速器上映射和调度嵌套循环程序时估算能耗，该方法不依赖问题规模，相比仿真方法具有更好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于仿真的能耗评估方法在分析嵌套循环程序在并行处理器阵列上的能耗时，存在可扩展性差的问题，特别是当迭代空间规模变化时，仿真时间会显著增加。因此需要一种能够快速评估映射和调度决策对能耗影响的方法，以支持设计空间探索。

Method: 提出了一种符号化能耗分析方法，通过数学建模捕获嵌套循环在处理器阵列上映射和调度决策对能耗的影响，避免了耗时的仿真过程。该方法独立于问题规模，能够快速分析不同映射调度方案的能耗。

Result: 在选定的基准测试和不同规模的迭代空间上，将符号化方法与基于仿真的结果进行对比验证。实验表明，仿真方法的计算开销随问题规模增长而显著增加，而本文提出的符号化分析方法与问题规模无关，具有更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出的符号化能耗分析方法为设计空间探索提供了一种高效的评估手段，可用于优化映射和调度决策、研究阵列规模变化的影响，以及与其他循环嵌套加速器架构进行对比分析，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This work presents a symbolic approach for estimating the energy consumption for nested loop programs when mapped and scheduled on parallel processor array accelerator architectures. Instead of simulation-based evaluation, we derive a methodology for symbolic energy analysis that captures the impact of mapping and scheduling decisions of loop nests on processor arrays. We compare our approach against simulation-based results for selected benchmarks and varying sizes of the iteration spaces. Whereas the latter are not scalable, our symbolic analysis is shown to be independent of the problem size. The presented evaluation methodology can be beneficially used during the design space exploration of mapping and scheduling decisions, for studying the influence of array size variations, and for comparisons with other loop nest accelerator architectures.

</details>

### [36] [Towards Realistic Waveform-Level IoT Network Simulation via IQ Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06408)
*Alexis Delplace,Samer Lahoud,Kinda Khawam,Dominique Quadri*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IQSim，一种基于IQ流混合的物联网网络仿真范式，通过在共享复基带IQ流中叠加传播处理后的波形并用软件接收机解调，解决了传统包级离散事件仿真无法捕捉波形级效应（如邻道泄漏、交调干扰）的问题，并验证了实时执行的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物联网网络仿真器大多采用包级离散事件系统，物理层行为通过解析干扰规则和预计算错误模型近似。这种虽然具有可扩展性，但会遗漏波形级效应（如邻道泄漏、共存信号间的交调干扰和接收机缺陷），而这些效应在异构sub-GHz ISM频段共存场景中至关重要。

Method: 引入IQSim仿真范式，基于同相/正交（IQ）流混合。不使用抽象碰撞模型预测数据包结果，而是维护一个共享的复基带IQStream，将仿真传输在传播处理后作为IQ波形插入，然后由软件接收机或硬件网关解调。工作流包括在线或离线波形生成、IQ域传播、波形叠加和向网关交付。

Result: 初步原型结果支持了该系统实时执行的可行性。

Conclusion: IQSim通过维护共享复基带IQ流并进行波形叠加与解调，能够更准确地捕捉关键波形级效应，为异构sub-GHz ISM频段共存场景提供了更真实的仿真手段，且具备实时执行潜力。

Abstract: Most Internet of Things (IoT) network simulators are packet-level discrete-event systems in which physical-layer (PHY) behavior is approximated through analytical interference rules and precomputed error models. While this enables scalable experiments, it can miss key waveform-level effects such as adjacent-channel leakage, cross-modulation interference between coexisting signals, and receiver imperfections, which are critical in heterogeneous sub-GHz ISM-band coexistence scenarios. This paper discusses these limitations and introduces IQSim, a simulation paradigm based on in-phase/quadrature (IQ) stream mixing. Instead of predicting packet outcomes from abstract collision models, IQSim maintains a shared complex baseband IQStream into which simulated transmissions are inserted as IQ waveforms after propagation processing, and then demodulated by software-based receivers or hardware gateways. We outline the end-to-end workflow, including online or offline waveform generation, IQ-domain propagation, waveform superposition, and delivery to gateways. We also report preliminary prototype results supporting the feasibility of real-time execution.

</details>
