*Tobias Falke,Nicolas Anastassacos,Samson Tan,Chankrisna Richy Meas,Chandana Satya Prakash,Nitesh Sekhar,M Saiful Bari,Krishna Kompella,Gamaleldin F. Elsayed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MoE路由测试平台，解决了专家专业化评估困难的问题，发现平衡范围是实现专家专业化和高利用率的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家架构在前沿大语言模型中日益流行，但路由复杂性带来了训练挑战。充分利用MoE模型参数需要所有专家都得到良好训练并以非冗余的方式专业化。然而，由于缺乏既定指标，且许多路由技术在较小规模下表现相似（往往不能反映大规模行为），评估专家专业化变得复杂。

Method: 提出MoE路由测试平台，该平台将具有清晰可区分领域的数据组合与基于这些领域规定理想路由的参考路由器配对，为比较提供明确定义的上界。该测试平台在小规模下提供对路由动态的更清晰可见性，同时使用真实数据，从而实现对专家专业化的可量化测量。

Result: 通过比较各种MoE路由方法，发现平衡范围是允许专业化同时保持高专家利用率的关键因素。该观察结果在模型规模扩大35倍后仍具有泛化性。

Conclusion: MoE路由测试平台为评估专家专业化提供了有效工具，揭示了平衡范围在路由策略中的重要性，该发现在不同规模模型中具有普遍适用性。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly popular for frontier large language models (LLM) but they introduce training challenges due to routing complexity. Fully leveraging parameters of an MoE model requires all experts to be well-trained and to specialize in non-redundant ways. Assessing this, however, is complicated due to lack of established metrics and, importantly, many routing techniques exhibit similar performance at smaller sizes, which is often not reflective of their behavior at large scale. To address this challenge, we propose the MoE Routing Testbed, a setup that gives clearer visibility into routing dynamics at small scale while using realistic data. The testbed pairs a data mix with clearly distinguishable domains with a reference router that prescribes ideal routing based on these domains, providing a well-defined upper bound for comparison. This enables quantifiable measurement of expert specialization. To demonstrate the value of the testbed, we compare various MoE routing approaches and show that balancing scope is the crucial factor that allows specialization while maintaining high expert utilization. We confirm that this observation generalizes to models 35x larger.

</details>


### [112] [Android Coach: Improve Online Agentic Training Efficiency with Single State Multiple Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07277)
*Guo Gan,Yuxuan Ding,Cong Chen,Yuwei Ren,Yin Huang,Hong Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Android Coach框架，通过将在线强化学习的训练范式从“单状态单动作”转变为“单状态多动作”，解决了Android代理在线训练成本高昂的问题，在提升成功率的同时显著提高了训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Android代理在线强化学习方法存在根本性局限：采用“单状态单动作”范式，仅利用在线单向采样中的状态-动作对更新策略，未能充分利用每个高延迟模拟器状态，导致在线交互指导学习成本过高。

Method: 提出Android Coach框架，实现“单状态多动作”训练范式。具体方法包括：1）学习一个价值评估器来估计动作价值，使智能体能够在单次在线状态下采样多个动作而不增加模拟器开销；2）集成过程奖励模型确保评估器可靠性；3）引入基于平均价值输出的组级优势估计器。

Result: 在AndroidLab和AndroidWorld数据集上，相比UI-TARS-1.5-7B基线模型，成功率分别提升7.5%和8.3%；在相同成功率条件下，训练效率比单状态单动作方法（PPO和GRPO）提高1.4倍。

Conclusion: Android Coach通过范式转换成功解决了Android代理在线强化学习的成本效率问题，证明了单状态多动作训练范式的有效性，为资源受限环境下的移动端智能体训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) serves as an effective method for enhancing the capabilities of Android agents. However, guiding agents to learn through online interaction is prohibitively expensive due to the high latency of emulators and the sample inefficiency of existing RL algorithms. We identify a fundamental limitation in current approaches: the Single State Single Action paradigm, which updates the policy with one-to-one state-action pairs from online one-way rollouts without fully exploring each costly emulator state. In this paper, we propose Android Coach, a novel framework that shifts the training paradigm to Single State Multiple Actions, allowing the agent to sample and utilize multiple actions for a single online state. We enable this without additional emulator overhead by learning a critic that estimates action values. To ensure the critic serves as a reliable coach, we integrate a process reward model and introduce a group-wise advantage estimator based on the averaged critic outputs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of Android Coach: it achieves 7.5% and 8.3% success rate improvements on AndroidLab and AndroidWorld over UI-TARS-1.5-7B, and attains 1.4x higher training efficiency than Single State Single Action methods PPO and GRPO at matched success rates.

</details>


### [113] [AdaBoost Does Not Always Cycle: A Computer-Assisted Counterexample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07055)
*Erik Y. Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个反例，证明了exhaustive AdaBoost并不总是收敛到有限循环，否定了Rudin等人在COLT 2012提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rudin、Schapire和Daubechies在COLT 2012提出了一个开放问题：exhaustive AdaBoost是否总是收敛到有限循环？本文旨在解决这一长期悬而未决的理论问题。

Method: 基于block-product gadget构造反例。该gadget的两个因子在5步分支映射下共享精确的周期2轨道，但其线性化返回映射的主特征值具有无理数的对数比值。这种无理性迫使burst-winner序列具有无理数的渐近频率，从而排除了最终的周期性。所有断言均通过精确有理算术进行验证。

Result: 成功构造了一个反例，证明exhaustive AdaBoost不一定收敛到有限循环。该反例通过精确的有理算术计算得到认证，burst-winner序列的无理渐近频率确保了非周期性。

Conclusion: exhaustive AdaBoost的收敛性比先前认为的更加复杂，存在不收敛到有限循环的情形，这加深了我们对Boosting算法动力学的理解。本研究是与GPT-5.4 Pro和Claude Opus 4.6协作完成的。

Abstract: We give a computer-assisted counterexample to the open question, posed by Rudin, Schapire, and Daubechies in COLT 2012, of whether exhaustive AdaBoost always converges to a finite cycle. The construction is based on a block-product gadget whose two factors share an exact period-2 orbit for their 5-step branch maps, but whose linearized return maps have dominant eigenvalues with an irrational logarithmic ratio. This irrationality forces the burst-winner sequence to have an irrational asymptotic frequency, precluding eventual periodicity. All assertions are certified by exact rational arithmetic. This work was developed in collaboration with GPT-5.4 Pro and Claude Opus 4.6.

</details>


### [114] [Production-Ready Automated ECU Calibration using Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07059)
*Andreas Kampmeier,Kevin Badalian,Lucas Koch,Sung-Yong Lee,Jakob Andert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于残差强化学习的可解释ECU自动标定方法，在HiL平台上验证了其能快速收敛到接近参考标定的结果，显著减少标定时间且几乎无需人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ECU标定依赖工程师手工设计参数，面临客户期望提高、开发周期缩短、法规要求增加、排放标准严格化以及车型变体增多等挑战，传统方法在实用性和经济性上逐渐失效。现有基于强化学习的方法虽能自动开发控制函数，但缺乏可解释性，难以在生产车辆中应用。

Method: 提出一种基于残差强化学习（residual RL）的可解释自动标定方法，遵循成熟的汽车开发原则。在硬件在环（HiL）平台上，以一个次优的map为起点，对基于map的空气路径控制器进行自动标定。

Result: 该方法能够快速收敛到与量产ECU中参考标定非常接近的结果，证明了其在工业应用中的适用性，能够在显著更短的时间内获得更好的标定结果，且几乎无需人工干预。

Conclusion: 所提出的可解释残差强化学习方法成功解决了传统标定方法的效率和成本问题，同时克服了传统强化学习方法缺乏可解释性的缺陷，为汽车工业提供了一种高效、自动化的ECU标定解决方案。

Abstract: Electronic Control Units (ECUs) have played a pivotal role in transforming motorcars of yore into the modern vehicles we see on our roads today. They actively regulate the actuation of individual components and thus determine the characteristics of the whole system. In this, the behavior of the control functions heavily depends on their calibration parameters which engineers traditionally design by hand. This is taking place in an environment of rising customer expectations and steadily shorter product development cycles. At the same time, legislative requirements are increasing while emission standards are getting stricter. Considering the number of vehicle variants on top of all that, the conventional method is losing its practical and financial viability. Prior work has already demonstrated that optimal control functions can be automatically developed with reinforcement learning (RL); since the resulting functions are represented by artificial neural networks, they lack explainability, a circumstance which renders them challenging to employ in production vehicles. In this article, we present an explainable approach to automating the calibration process using residual RL which follows established automotive development principles. Its applicability is demonstrated by means of a map-based air path controller in a series control unit using a hardware-in-the-loop (HiL) platform. Starting with a sub-optimal map, the proposed methodology quickly converges to a calibration which closely resembles the reference in the series ECU. The results prove that the approach is suitable for the industry where it leads to better calibrations in significantly less time and requires virtually no human intervention

</details>


### [115] [Epistemic Robust Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07072)
*Abhilash Reddy Chenreddy,Erick Delage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑的不确定性集合框架，用于解决离线强化学习中因数据覆盖有限而导致的认知不确定性问题，相比传统集成方法需要更小的模型规模，并在风险敏感行为策略基准测试中表现出更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习面临认知不确定性的关键挑战，这源于有限或有偏差的数据覆盖，特别是当行为策略系统性地避免某些动作时，会导致不准确的价值估计和不可靠的泛化。现有集成方法（如SAC-N）虽然通过使用集成最小值来保守估计Q值来缓解此问题，但需要大型集成，并且经常混淆认知不确定性和随机不确定性。

Method: 提出一个统一且可泛化的框架，用紧凑的Q值不确定性集合替代离散集成。该框架直接塑造不确定性集合，以在鲁棒贝尔曼目标下优化累积奖励，而无需依赖集成。同时引入了一个用于评估风险敏感行为策略下离线强化学习算法的基准。

Result: 在表格和连续状态域的实验表明，该方法相比基于集成的基线方法实现了更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力，同时需要更小的模型规模。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑不确定性集合框架有效地解决了离线强化学习中的认知不确定性问题，在减少计算开销的同时提高了算法的鲁棒性和泛化性能，为离线强化学习提供了一个更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning learns policies from fixed datasets without further environment interaction. A key challenge in this setting is epistemic uncertainty, arising from limited or biased data coverage, particularly when the behavior policy systematically avoids certain actions. This can lead to inaccurate value estimates and unreliable generalization. Ensemble-based methods like SAC-N mitigate this by conservatively estimating Q-values using the ensemble minimum, but they require large ensembles and often conflate epistemic with aleatoric uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose a unified and generalizable framework that replaces discrete ensembles with compact uncertainty sets over Q-values. %We further introduce an Epinet based model that directly shapes the uncertainty sets to optimize the cumulative reward under the robust Bellman objective without relying on ensembles. We also introduce a benchmark for evaluating offline RL algorithms under risk-sensitive behavior policies, and demonstrate that our method achieves improved robustness and generalization over ensemble-based baselines across both tabular and continuous state domains.

</details>


### [116] [Mining Electronic Health Records to Investigate Effectiveness of Ensemble Deep Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07085)
*Manar D. Samad,Yina Hou,Shrabani Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于集成的深度聚类方法，结合传统聚类，在心衰患者EHR数据的聚类任务中取得了最佳性能，强调了结合传统与深度方法的优势以及性别特异性聚类的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电子健康记录（EHR）患者聚类与疾病亚型区分主要依赖传统方法（如K-means）或自编码器与聚类结合的混合方法，但深度聚类方法主要针对图像设计，在表格型EHR数据上表现受限。因此，需要针对EHR数据特点改进深度聚类方法，并探索更有效的聚类框架。

Method: 本文在All of Us Research Program的真实心衰患者EHR数据上，系统评估了传统、混合与深度学习三类共14种聚类方法。针对深度聚类的不足，提出了一种基于集成的深度聚类方法，聚合多个嵌入维度的聚类分配，而非依赖单一固定嵌入空间。进一步，将此方法与传统聚类结合，构建了一种新颖的集成框架。

Result: 在多组患者队列上，所提出的集成嵌入深度聚类方法结合传统聚类后，在所有14种聚类方法中取得了最佳的整体性能排名。研究发现，专为图像设计的深度学习方法在表格型EHR数据上不如传统方法稳健。此外，研究突出了按生物学性别特异性进行EHR数据聚类的重要性。

Conclusion: 在EHR数据聚类任务中，结合传统聚类与深度聚类的集成方法优于单一方法。针对EHR数据特点设计的集成嵌入深度聚类方法有效提升了性能。未来的EHR聚类研究应重视性别特异性分析，并考虑采用集成策略以获得更稳健的结果。

Abstract: In electronic health records (EHRs), clustering patients and distinguishing disease subtypes are key tasks to elucidate pathophysiology and aid clinical decision-making. However, clustering in healthcare informatics is still based on traditional methods, especially K-means, and has achieved limited success when applied to embedding representations learned by autoencoders as hybrid methods. This paper investigates the effectiveness of traditional, hybrid, and deep learning methods in heart failure patient cohorts using real EHR data from the All of Us Research Program. Traditional clustering methods perform robustly because deep learning approaches are specifically designed for image clustering, a task that differs substantially from the tabular EHR data setting. To address the shortcomings of deep clustering, we introduce an ensemble-based deep clustering approach that aggregates cluster assignments obtained from multiple embedding dimensions, rather than relying on a single fixed embedding space. When combined with traditional clustering in a novel ensemble framework, the proposed ensemble embedding for deep clustering delivers the best overall performance ranking across 14 diverse clustering methods and multiple patient cohorts. This paper underscores the importance of biological sex-specific clustering of EHR data and the advantages of combining traditional and deep clustering approaches over a single method.

</details>


### [117] [Are Stochastic Multi-objective Bandits Harder than Single-objective Bandits?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07096)
*Changkun Guan,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多目标老虎机是否比单目标更难这一长期争议，证明了随机设定下帕累托遗憾由最大次优性差距主导，并提出了最优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标老虎机中奖励为多维向量，引入了帕累托序关系和帕累托遗憾。已有研究在对抗性设定下证明帕累托遗憾不超过经典遗憾，但在随机设定下结论尚不明确，甚至有研究指出其随维度增加而增大。这引发了核心问题：多目标老虎机是否本质上比单目标更难优化？

Method: 提出了一个基于双层不确定性量化的新算法。算法在臂和目标两个层面上通过上下置信界估计器进行不确定性量化，结合了用于臂选择的前二竞赛策略（top-two racing）和用于维度选择的不确定性贪婪规则（uncertainty-greedy rule），从而在两个层面上平衡探索与利用。

Result: 证明了在随机设定下，帕累托遗憾由最大次优性差距\(g^\dagger\)主导，其下界为\(\Omega(\frac{K\log T}{g^\dagger})\)。提出的算法实现了\(O(\frac{K\log T}{g^\dagger})\)的帕累托遗憾，达到了最优性。全面数值实验验证了算法的遗憾保证，并在基准方法上表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 多目标老虎机在随机设定下并不比单目标更难，其帕累托遗憾由最大次优性差距主导而非维度。通过双层不确定性量化和前二竞赛策略的结合，可以实现最优的帕累托遗憾界。

Abstract: Multi-objective bandits have attracted increasing attention because of their broad applicability and mathematical elegance, where the reward of each arm is a multi-dimensional vector rather than a scalar. This naturally introduces Pareto order relations and Pareto regret. A long-standing question in this area is whether performance is fundamentally harder to optimize because of this added complexity. A recent surprising result shows that, in the adversarial setting, Pareto regret is no larger than classical regret; however, in the stochastic setting, where the regret notion is different, the picture remains unclear. In fact, existing work suggests that Pareto regret in the stochastic case increases with the dimensionality. This controversial yet subtle phenomenon motivates our central question: \emph{are multi-objective bandits actually harder than single-objective ones?} We answer this question in full by showing that, in the stochastic setting, Pareto regret is in fact governed by the maximum sub-optimality gap \(g^\dagger\), and hence by the minimum marginal regret of order \(Ω(\frac{K\log T}{g^\dagger})\). We further develop a new algorithm that achieves Pareto regret of order \(O(\frac{K\log T}{g^\dagger})\), and is therefore optimal. The algorithm leverages a nested two-layer uncertainty quantification over both arms and objectives through upper and lower confidence bound estimators. It combines a top-two racing strategy for arm selection with an uncertainty-greedy rule for dimension selection. Together, these components balance exploration and exploitation across the two layers. We also conduct comprehensive numerical experiments to validate the proposed algorithm, showing the desired regret guarantee and significant gains over benchmark methods.

</details>


### [118] [Selective Neuron Amplification for Training-Free Task Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07098)
*Ryyan Akhtar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了选择性神经元放大（SNA）方法，通过在推理时增强任务相关神经元的影响力来改善大语言模型的性能，发现部分模型失败是由于激活过弱而非能力缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常在看似已理解的任务上失败，研究发现这种失败更多源于推理过程中某些内部回路激活不足，而非模型知识缺失。

Method: 提出选择性神经元放大（Selective Neuron Amplification, SNA）方法，该方法在推理时工作，通过增加任务相关神经元的影响力来改善模型表现，且不改变模型参数。

Result: SNA主要在模型不确定时起作用，而在模型已经自信时影响较小。这表明一些模型失败是由于激活强度不足而非能力缺乏。

Conclusion: 部分大语言模型的失败可以通过增强弱激活的神经元来缓解，这挑战了模型失败必然意味着能力不足的传统观点。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on tasks they seem to already understand. In our experiments, this appears to be less about missing knowledge and more about certain internal circuits not being strongly activated during inference. We explore Selective Neuron Amplification, which increases the influence of task relevant neurons without changing the model's parameters. The method works at inference time and does not permanently alter the model. SNA helps mainly when the model is uncertain, while having low effect when the model is already confident. This suggests that some model failures are due to weak activation rather than lack of capability.

</details>


### [119] [Lumbermark: Resistant Clustering by Chopping Up Mutual Reachability Minimum Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07143)
*Marek Gagolewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Lumbermark算法，这是一种鲁棒的分裂聚类算法，能够检测不同大小、密度和形状的簇，可作为HDBSCAN的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有聚类算法在检测不同大小、密度和形状的簇时的局限性，并提供一种能够产生用户指定大小分区的HDBSCAN替代方法。

Method: 采用分裂聚类策略，通过迭代地切割数据集互可达性最小生成树中由突出段连接的大分支来进行聚类；使用互可达性距离平滑数据分布，降低低密度对象（如簇间噪声点或边缘异常值）的影响。

Result: 该方法在基准数据上表现良好，并提供了开源的Python和R语言实现包'lumbermark'，便于数据科学家和从业者使用。

Conclusion: Lumbermark算法是一种有效且实用的聚类方法，能够在各种场景下检测不同特征的簇，为不同领域的数据科学家和从业者提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: We introduce Lumbermark, a robust divisive clustering algorithm capable of detecting clusters of varying sizes, densities, and shapes. Lumbermark iteratively chops off large limbs connected by protruding segments of a dataset's mutual reachability minimum spanning tree. The use of mutual reachability distances smoothens the data distribution and decreases the influence of low-density objects, such as noise points between clusters or outliers at their peripheries. The algorithm can be viewed as an alternative to HDBSCAN that produces partitions with user-specified sizes. A fast, easy-to-use implementation of the new method is available in the open-source 'lumbermark' package for Python and R. We show that Lumbermark performs well on benchmark data and hope it will prove useful to data scientists and practitioners across different fields.

</details>


### [120] [Multi-Turn Reasoning LLMs for Task Offloading in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07148)
*Ning Yang,Chuangxin Cheng,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COMLLM框架，利用大语言模型解决移动边缘计算中的任务卸载问题，实现了零样本拓扑扩展能力和近优延迟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动边缘计算中的任务卸载面临动态任务到达、时变信道和服务器队列时空耦合等挑战。传统启发式方法缺乏适应性，深度强化学习存在泛化能力差和架构刚性问题，而标准监督微调(SFT)导致短视策略，只关注即时延迟最小化而忽视系统长期演化。

Method: 提出COMLLM生成式框架，集成群体相对策略优化(GRPO)与前瞻协同仿真(LACS)机制。LACS执行多步蒙特卡洛推演并联合建模服务器队列动态，将推演结果融入奖励设计中，从而捕捉当前决策对未来系统状态的长期影响。

Result: COMLLM实现了近优延迟和改进的负载均衡公平性。最重要的是，展现出零样本拓扑扩展性：在小规模网络上训练的模型能够泛化到更大、未见过的拓扑结构，无需重新训练，性能优于SFT、DRL和启发式基线。

Conclusion: COMLLM成功解决了传统方法的局限性，通过前瞻性决策机制实现了对长期系统演化的建模，为零样本拓扑扩展的边缘计算任务卸载提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Emerging computation-intensive applications impose stringent latency requirements on resource-constrained mobile devices. Mobile Edge Computing (MEC) addresses this challenge through task offloading. However, designing effective policies remains difficult due to dynamic task arrivals, time-varying channels, and the spatio-temporal coupling of server queues. Conventional heuristics lack adaptability, while Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) suffers from limited generalization and architectural rigidity, requiring retraining when network topology changes. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) offer semantic reasoning capabilities, standard Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) yields myopic policies that greedily minimize immediate latency without accounting for long-term system evolution. To address these limitations, we propose COMLLM, a generative framework that enables foresighted decision-making in MEC systems. COMLLM integrates Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a Look-Ahead Collaborative Simulation (LACS) mechanism, which performs multi-step Monte Carlo rollouts while jointly modeling server queue dynamics. By incorporating these rollouts into the reward design, the framework captures the long-term impact of current decisions on future system states. Experimental results demonstrate that COMLLM achieves near-optimal latency and improved load-balancing fairness. Notably, it exhibits zero-shot topological scalability, allowing a model trained on small-scale networks to generalize to larger, unseen topologies without retraining, outperforming SFT, DRL, and heuristic baselines.

</details>


### [121] [SBBTS: A Unified Schrödinger-Bass Framework for Synthetic Financial Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07159)
*Alexandre Alouadi,Grégoire Loeper,Célian Marsala,Othmane Mazhar,Huyên Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SBBTS框架，通过扩展Schrödinger-Bass公式解决时间序列生成中漂移和波动率的联合建模问题，在金融数据上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机器学习中，生成既能复现边际分布又能复现时间动态的合成时间序列是一个核心挑战。现有方法（如基于扩散的方法固定波动率，或鞅传输模型忽略漂移）无法同时建模漂移和随机波动率。

Method: 引入时间序列Schrödinger-Bass桥（SBBTS）统一框架。该方法扩展了Schrödinger-Bass公式以处理多步时间序列，构建了一个联合校准漂移和波动率的扩散过程，并可分解为条件传输问题以实现高效学习。

Result: 在Heston模型上的数值实验表明，SBBTS能准确恢复先前Schrödinger Bridge方法无法捕获的随机波动率和相关参数。应用于S&P 500数据时，使用SBBTS生成的合成时间序列进行数据增强，持续提升了下游预测性能，相比仅使用真实数据训练，获得了更高的分类准确率和夏普比率。

Conclusion: SBBTS为金融应用中的现实时间序列生成和数据增强提供了一个实用且有效的框架。

Abstract: We study the problem of generating synthetic time series that reproduce both marginal distributions and temporal dynamics, a central challenge in financial machine learning. Existing approaches typically fail to jointly model drift and stochastic volatility, as diffusion-based methods fix the volatility while martingale transport models ignore drift. We introduce the Schrödinger-Bass Bridge for Time Series (SBBTS), a unified framework that extends the Schrödinger-Bass formulation to multi-step time series. The method constructs a diffusion process that jointly calibrates drift and volatility and admits a tractable decomposition into conditional transport problems, enabling efficient learning. Numerical experiments on the Heston model demonstrate that SBBTS accurately recovers stochastic volatility and correlation parameters that prior SchrödingerBridge methods fail to capture. Applied to S&P 500 data, SBBTS-generated synthetic time series consistently improve downstream forecasting performance when used for data augmentation, yielding higher classification accuracy and Sharpe ratio compared to real-data-only training. These results show that SBBTS provides a practical and effective framework for realistic time series generation and data augmentation in financial applications.

</details>


### [122] [Smart Commander: A Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning Framework for Fleet-Level PHM Decision Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07171)
*Yong Si,Mingfei Lu,Jing Li,Yang Hu,Guijiang Li,Yueheng Song,Zhaokui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Smart Commander，一种分层强化学习（HRL）框架，用于解决军用航空PHM中大规模机队行动面临的“维度灾难”、反馈稀疏和随机任务剖面等挑战，实现了对维护与物流决策的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 军用航空 prognostics and health management (PHM) 在大规模机队行动中面临“维度灾难”问题，同时存在反馈稀疏和任务剖面具有随机性等显著挑战，导致决策制定困难。

Method: 提出了一种名为Smart Commander的分层强化学习（HRL）框架。该框架将复杂的控制问题分解为两层层级：战略层的“总指挥官”负责管理机队级别的可用性和成本目标；战术层的“作战指挥官”负责执行具体行动，如出动生成、维护调度和资源分配。方法通过整合分层奖励塑形与规划增强的神经网络，有效解决了稀疏和延迟奖励带来的训练困难，并在自建的高保真离散事件仿真环境中进行了验证。

Result: 实验评估表明，Smart Commander显著优于传统的单体深度强化学习（DRL）和基于规则的基准方法。值得注意的是，该方法在大幅减少训练时间的同时，在易发生故障的环境中展现了卓越的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了分层强化学习（HRL）作为下一代智能机队管理可靠范式的潜力。

Abstract: Decision-making in military aviation Prognostics and Health Management (PHM) faces significant challenges due to the "curse of dimensionality" in large-scale fleet operations, combined with sparse feedback and stochastic mission profiles. To address these issues, this paper proposes Smart Commander, a novel Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) framework designed to optimize sequential maintenance and logistics decisions. The framework decomposes the complex control problem into a two-tier hierarchy: a strategic General Commander manages fleet-level availability and cost objectives, while tactical Operation Commanders execute specific actions for sortie generation, maintenance scheduling, and resource allocation. The proposed approach is validated within a custom-built, high-fidelity discrete-event simulation environment that captures the dynamics of aircraft configuration and support logistics.By integrating layered reward shaping with planning-enhanced neural networks, the method effectively addresses the difficulty of sparse and delayed rewards. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that Smart Commander significantly outperforms conventional monolithic Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) and rule-based baselines. Notably, it achieves a substantial reduction in training time while demonstrating superior scalability and robustness in failure-prone environments. These results highlight the potential of HRL as a reliable paradigm for next-generation intelligent fleet management.

</details>


### [123] [Improving Semantic Uncertainty Quantification in Language Model Question-Answering via Token-Level Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07172)
*Tom A. Lamb,Desi R. Ivanova,Philip H. S. Torr,Tim G. J. Rudner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对语义不确定性量化中被忽视的校准问题，提出了一种简单有效的单标量温度缩放方法，显著提升了语义置信度的校准性能和判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义不确定性量化工作主要关注判别能力而忽视了校准，但这两者捕捉了不确定性的不同方面。仅关注判别会导致对模型不确定性的理解不完整，因此需要系统性地评估和改进校准性能。

Method: 提出优化单一标量温度参数的方法进行校准。作者论证单一标量温度提供了合适的归纳偏置，相比固定的温度启发式方法和更具表达力的token级重校准方法更为有效。

Result: 在问答任务上的全面评估表明，温度缩放方法一致地改善了语义校准、判别能力和下游熵，优于启发式基线和更具表达力的token级重校准方法。当前方法（尤其是固定温度启发式方法）产生了系统性校准不良和判别能力差的语义置信度分布。

Conclusion: 单一标量温度优化是一种简单但有效的语义置信度校准解决方案，能够同时提升校准性能和判别能力，为语义不确定性量化提供了更可靠的置信度估计。

Abstract: Calibration is central to reliable semantic uncertainty quantification, yet prior work has largely focused on discrimination, neglecting calibration. As calibration and discrimination capture distinct aspects of uncertainty, focusing on discrimination alone yields an incomplete picture. We address this gap by systematically evaluating both aspects across a broad set of confidence measures. We show that current approaches, particularly fixed-temperature heuristics, produce systematically miscalibrated and poorly discriminative semantic confidence distributions. We demonstrate that optimising a single scalar temperature, which, we argue, provides a suitable inductive bias, is a surprisingly simple yet effective solution. Our exhaustive evaluation confirms that temperature scaling consistently improves semantic calibration, discrimination, and downstream entropy, outperforming both heuristic baselines and more expressive token-level recalibration methods on question-answering tasks.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond the Mean: Modelling Annotation Distributions in Continuous Affect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07198)
*Kosmas Pinitas,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Beta分布的分布感知框架，用于连续情感预测。该方法不再预测单一数值，而是估计标注分布的均值和标准差，从而能够捕捉标注者之间的分歧、不确定性和分布形态，在SEWA和RECOLA数据集上取得了与传统回归方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感标注本质上具有主观性和认知复杂性，不同标注者对同一信号会产生多样化的感知。现有的连续情感预测通常将这种变异性简化为均值或中位数等点估计，从而丢弃了关于标注者分歧和不确定性的宝贵信息。因此，需要一种能够保留并建模这种分布信息的方法。

Method: 提出了一种分布感知框架，利用Beta分布对标注共识进行建模。模型不再预测单一的情感值，而是估计标注分布的均值和标准差，并通过矩匹配将其转换为有效的Beta分布参数。这种公式化方法能够以封闭形式恢复高阶分布描述符，包括偏度、峰度和分位数。

Result: 在SEWA和RECOLA数据集上使用多模态特征进行了评估。实验结果表明，基于Beta分布的建模方法生成的预测分布与实际标注分布高度吻合，同时在与传统回归方法的性能对比中保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在情感计算中建模标注不确定性的重要性，证明了分布感知学习在主观信号分析中的潜力。该方法不仅捕捉了情感感知的集中趋势，还捕获了标注者响应中的变异性、不对称性和不确定性。

Abstract: Emotion annotation is inherently subjective and cognitively demanding, producing signals that reflect diverse perceptions across annotators rather than a single ground truth. In continuous affect prediction, this variability is typically collapsed into point estimates such as the mean or median, discarding valuable information about annotator disagreement and uncertainty. In this work, we propose a distribution-aware framework that models annotation consensus using the Beta distribution. Instead of predicting a single affect value, models estimate the mean and standard deviation of the annotation distribution, which are transformed into valid Beta parameters through moment matching. This formulation enables the recovery of higher-order distributional descriptors, including skewness, kurtosis, and quantiles, in closed form. As a result, the model captures not only the central tendency of emotional perception but also variability, asymmetry, and uncertainty in annotator responses. We evaluate the proposed approach on the SEWA and RECOLA datasets using multimodal features. Experimental results show that Beta-based modelling produces predictive distributions that closely match the empirical annotator distributions while achieving competitive performance with conventional regression approaches. These findings highlight the importance of modelling annotation uncertainty in affective computing and demonstrate the potential of distribution-aware learning for subjective signal analysis.

</details>


### [125] [Diffusion Processes on Implicit Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07213)
*Victor Kawasaki-Borruat,Clara Grotehans,Pierre Vandergheynst,Adam Gosztolai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了隐式流形扩散（IMDs），一种仅从点云样本出发、在环境空间中构建数据流形上扩散过程的方法，并证明了该过程在概率路径空间上收敛于光滑流形上的对应过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据通常分布在低维流形附近。现有的扩散过程构建方法通常依赖于图表、投影或其他几何基元，而在实际应用中往往只能获取点云样本，缺乏对底层流形的显式几何信息。因此，研究如何在隐式设置下（即仅使用点云样本）构建数据流形上的扩散过程具有重要意义。

Method: 该方法从数据构建邻近图，以此估计扩散过程的无穷小生成元及其Carré-du-champ（CDC）。生成元与CDC共同编码了目标扩散的局部随机与几何结构，从而定义了一个数据驱动的随机微分方程（SDE），使其在环境空间中刻画流形上的内在扩散。数值模拟采用Euler-Maruyama积分方案实现。

Result: 证明了随着样本数量的增加，所诱导的扩散过程在概率路径空间上依分布收敛于光滑流形上的对应扩散过程，并给出了可行的数值模拟算法。

Conclusion: IMDs为在数据流形上实现扩散动力学提供了严格的理论基础，支持流形感知的采样、探索与生成建模等应用的实践实现，并开启了相关领域的新研究方向。

Abstract: High-dimensional data are often modeled as lying near a low-dimensional manifold. We study how to construct diffusion processes on this data manifold in the implicit setting. That is, using only point cloud samples and without access to charts, projections, or other geometric primitives. Our main contribution is a data-driven SDE that captures intrinsic diffusion on the underlying manifold while being defined in ambient space. The construction relies on estimating the diffusion's infinitesimal generator and its carré-du-champ (CDC) from a proximity graph built from the data. The generator and CDC together encode the local stochastic and geometric structure of the intended diffusion. We show that, as the number of samples grows, the induced process converges in law on the space of probability paths to its smooth manifold counterpart. We call this construction Implicit Manifold-valued Diffusions (IMDs), and furthermore present a numerical simulation procedure using Euler-Maruyama integration. This gives a rigorous basis for practical implementations of diffusion dynamics on data manifolds, and opens new directions for manifold-aware sampling, exploration, and generative modeling.

</details>


### [126] [How Does Machine Learning Manage Complexity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07233)
*Lance Fortnow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从计算复杂性视角分析了机器学习模型的能力，通过将机器学习抽象为产生P/poly可计算分布的过程，揭示了其在建模复杂系统（特别是密码学伪随机生成器）时的内在限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型通常在来自可采样或更复杂分布的数据上进行训练，这比仅限于可计算分布的范围要广泛得多。作者希望通过关注可计算分布，使机器学习模型能够更好地通过概率来管理复杂性，从而提供一个计算复杂性视角来理解机器学习模型的能力。

Method: 研究者抽象掉具体的学习机制，将机器学习建模为产生具有多项式有界最大熵的P/poly可计算分布的过程。通过这种抽象，他们分析了机器学习模型在处理复杂分布时的行为。

Result: 研究表明，如果一个机器学习模型产生一个分布μ，该分布能够最小化由密码学伪随机生成器生成的分布的误差，那么μ必须接近均匀分布。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解机器学习模型的能力提供了一个计算复杂性视角，特别是在建模复杂系统方面。通过将机器学习模型抽象为产生可计算分布的过程，可以更好地理解其在处理复杂分布时的行为和限制。

Abstract: We provide a computational complexity lens to understand the power of machine learning models, particularly their ability to model complex systems.
  Machine learning models are often trained on data drawn from sampleable or more complex distributions, a far wider range of distributions than just computable ones. By focusing on computable distributions, machine learning models can better manage complexity via probability. We abstract away from specific learning mechanisms, modeling machine learning as producing P/poly-computable distributions with polynomially-bounded max-entropy.
  We illustrate how learning computable distributions models complexity by showing that if a machine learning model produces a distribution $μ$ that minimizes error against the distribution generated by a cryptographic pseudorandom generator, then $μ$ must be close to uniform.

</details>


### [127] [On the Price of Privacy for Language Identification and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07238)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在不可知统计设定下研究差分隐私（DP）语言识别与生成的代价。研究发现近似DP（ε,δ-DP）可达到与非隐私情形相同的错误率，而纯DP（ε-DP）下错误率指数仅恶化一个min{1,ε}因子，该界是紧的。结论是隐私代价非常温和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地在敏感用户数据上训练，理解语言学习中隐私保护的基本代价变得至关重要。现有研究缺乏对差分隐私下语言学习任务的系统性理论分析，因此需要建立算法与匹配的下界来精确量化隐私代价。

Method: 在不可知统计设定下，针对差分隐私语言识别和生成两个任务，分别设计算法并推导理论上界；同时证明匹配的下界。通过分析近似(ε,δ)-DP和纯ε-DP两种隐私模型下的错误率指数形式，定量比较与非隐私情形的差距。

Result: 对于近似(ε,δ)-DP（常数ε>0），可恢复非隐私错误率：识别任务为exp(-r(n))（任意r(n)=o(n)），生成任务为exp(-Ω(n))。对于纯ε-DP，错误率指数恶化一个乘性因子min{1,ε}，且该因子在常数范围内是紧的。在温和假设下，生成的上界与下界匹配，达到最优率。

Conclusion: 差分隐私在语言学习中的代价出人意料地温和：在近似DP下代价完全消失，在纯DP下仅体现为指数项中精确的min{1,ε}因子。这为在LLM训练中应用隐私保护提供了理论基础和乐观信号。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained on sensitive user data, understanding the fundamental cost of privacy in language learning becomes essential. We initiate the study of differentially private (DP) language identification and generation in the agnostic statistical setting, establishing algorithms and matching lower bounds that precisely quantify the cost of privacy. For both tasks, approximate $(\varepsilon, δ)$-DP with constant $\varepsilon > 0$ recovers the non-private error rates: $\exp(-r(n))$ for identification (for any $r(n) = o(n)$) and $\exp(-Ω(n))$ for generation. Under pure $\varepsilon$-DP, the exponents degrade by a multiplicative factor of $\min\{1, \varepsilon\}$, which we show is tight up to constants. Notably, for generation under pure DP with mild assumptions, the upper bound $\exp(-\min\{1,\varepsilon\} \cdot Ω(n))$ matches the lower bound up to some constants, establishing an optimal rate. Our results show that the cost of privacy in language learning is surprisingly mild: absent entirely under approximate DP, and exactly a $\min\{1,\varepsilon\}$ factor in the exponent under pure DP.

</details>


### [128] [Weaves, Wires, and Morphisms: Formalizing and Implementing the Algebra of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07242)
*Vincent Abbott,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于范畴论的深度学习模型形式化框架，通过引入轴步长和数组广播范畴来精确描述广播机制，实现了模型架构的数学定义、组合操作与可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习模型执行的是明确的数学函数，但目前缺乏描述模型架构的形式化数学框架。现有的临时符号、图表和伪代码难以处理非线性广播问题，也无法很好地表达单个组件与组合模型之间的关系。

Method: 提出一个基于范畴论的深度学习模型框架，引入两个新颖的范畴：轴步长范畴（axis-stride category）和数组广播范畴（array-broadcasted category），以此形式化广播机制。该方法将数学定义转化为人类可读的图表和机器可管理的数据结构，并提供了Python（pyncd）和TypeScript（tsncd）的镜像实现。

Result: 成功构建了一个通用的形式化框架，实现了代数构造、图转换、PyTorch编译和图表渲染等功能。该框架允许以组合方式精确表达和操作架构底层的数学函数。

Conclusion: 该工作为深度学习模型的设计与分析奠定了系统化、形式化方法的基础，弥补了现有方法在处理广播机制和组合性方面的不足。

Abstract: Despite deep learning models running well-defined mathematical functions, we lack a formal mathematical framework for describing model architectures. Ad-hoc notation, diagrams, and pseudocode poorly handle nonlinear broadcasting and the relationship between individual components and composed models. This paper introduces a categorical framework for deep learning models that formalizes broadcasting through the novel axis-stride and array-broadcasted categories. This allows the mathematical function underlying architectures to be precisely expressed and manipulated in a compositional manner. These mathematical definitions are translated into human manageable diagrams and machine manageable data structures. We provide a mirrored implementation in Python (pyncd) and TypeScript (tsncd) to show the universal aspect of our framework, along with features including algebraic construction, graph conversion, PyTorch compilation and diagram rendering. This lays the foundation for a systematic, formal approach to deep learning model design and analysis.

</details>


### [129] [A comparative analysis of machine learning models in SHAP analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07258)
*Justin Lin,Julia Fukuyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文详细研究了SHAP分析方法在不同机器学习模型和数据集上的应用特性，并提出了针对多分类问题的瀑布图新型泛化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型黑箱模型的普及，其缺乏可解释性导致在高风险场景中不可信且使用受限。SHAP分析作为一种可解释AI方法虽日益流行，但其解释依赖于模型，缺乏通用的分析流程，需要深入研究以指导分析人员。

Method: 对SHAP分析在各种机器学习模型和数据集上进行了详细的实验研究与分析，揭示了SHAP分析的细节与细微差别，并提出了一种将瀑布图推广到多分类问题的新方法。

Result: 揭示了SHAP分析在不同模型下的具体特性与细节，为分析人员在该领域的探索提供了指导和洞见，同时成功实现了瀑布图在多分类问题上的泛化。

Conclusion: 通过对SHAP分析的深入研究，赋能分析人员更好地理解和应用这一可解释性工具，为黑箱模型的可解释性分析提供了有价值的参考和方法支持。

Abstract: In this growing age of data and technology, large black-box models are becoming the norm due to their ability to handle vast amounts of data and learn incredibly complex data patterns. The deficiency of these methods, however, is their inability to explain the prediction process, making them untrustworthy and their use precarious in high-stakes situations. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) analysis is an explainable AI method growing in popularity for its ability to explain model predictions in terms of the original features. For each sample and feature in the data set, an associated SHAP value quantifies the contribution of that feature to the prediction of that sample. Analysis of these SHAP values provides valuable insight into the model's decision-making process, which can be leveraged to create data-driven solutions. The interpretation of these SHAP values, however, is model-dependent, so there does not exist a universal analysis procedure. To aid in these efforts, we present a detailed investigation of SHAP analysis across various machine learning models and data sets. In uncovering the details and nuance behind SHAP analysis, we hope to empower analysts in this less-explored territory. We also present a novel generalization of the waterfall plot to the multi-classification problem.

</details>


### [130] [Tracking Adaptation Time: Metrics for Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07266)
*Lorenzo Iovine,Giacomo Ziffer,Emanuele Della Valle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种互补的指标，用于在时序分布偏移下区分模型适应性失败与数据内在难度，揭示了现有分析无法发现的适应性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估指标仅量化性能的平均下降，无法区分模型是未能适应演化数据，还是数据本身变得更具内在挑战性，导致对时序退化的误判。

Method: 提出三种互补的指标，用于区分模型的自适应能力与数据的内在难度，从而提供对时序分布偏移下模型行为的动态且可解释的视角。

Result: 实验结果表明，新提出的指标能够揭示被现有分析方法所掩盖的适应性模式，为演化环境中的时序鲁棒性提供了更丰富的理解。

Conclusion: 该方法改进了对时序分布偏移下模型鲁棒性的评估，解决了现有指标无法区分适应性与数据难度的局限性。

Abstract: Evaluating robustness under temporal distribution shift remains an open challenge. Existing metrics quantify the average decline in performance, but fail to capture how models adapt to evolving data. As a result, temporal degradation is often misinterpreted: when accuracy declines, it is unclear whether the model is failing to adapt or whether the data itself has become inherently more challenging to learn. In this work, we propose three complementary metrics to distinguish adaptation from intrinsic difficulty in the data. Together, these metrics provide a dynamic and interpretable view of model behavior under temporal distribution shift. Results show that our metrics uncover adaptation patterns hidden by existing analysis, offering a richer understanding of temporal robustness in evolving environments.

</details>


### [131] [Graph Neural ODE Digital Twins for Control-Oriented Reactor Thermal-Hydraulic Forecasting Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07292)
*Akzhol Almukhametov,Doyeong Lim,Rui Hu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于先进反应堆实时监督控制的物理信息图神经网络与神经常微分方程耦合（GNN-ODE）代理模型，实现了对未监测节点热工水力状态的高精度、毫秒级推理预测，并具备从仿真向实验数据迁移的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进反应堆的实时监督控制需要对全厂热工水力状态进行精确预测，尤其是物理传感器无法覆盖的位置。现有需求呼唤一种既能保证预测保真度、又具备毫秒级推理速度、且对部分可观测性具有鲁棒性的代理模型。

Method: 将整个系统表示为有向传感器图，边通过流动/传热感知的消息传递编码水力连接性；利用受控神经常微分方程在连续时间内推进潜在动力学；设计拓扑引导的缺失节点初始化器在推演开始时重建未监测状态，随后进行完全自回归预测；采用分层判别式微调方法实现仿真向实验数据的迁移。

Result: 在保留仿真瞬态测试中，未监测节点的平均MAE在60秒时为0.91 K，300秒时为2.18 K，缺失节点状态重建的R²高达0.995；单GPU推理速度约为模拟时间的105倍，支持64成员集成推演用于不确定性量化；仅用30个训练序列完成仿真到实验数据的迁移，学习到的流动相关传热标度恢复的雷诺数指数与既有关联式一致。

Conclusion: 该GNN-ODE代理模型成功同时满足了预测保真度、毫秒级推理和部分可观测性鲁棒性三项核心要求，能够准确跟踪剧烈功率变化瞬态并预测未监测位置的轨迹，为先进反应堆实时监督控制提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time supervisory control of advanced reactors requires accurate forecasting of plant-wide thermal-hydraulic states, including locations where physical sensors are unavailable. Meeting this need calls for surrogate models that combine predictive fidelity, millisecond-scale inference, and robustness to partial observability. In this work, we present a physics-informed message-passing Graph Neural Network coupled with a Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (GNN-ODE) to addresses all three requirements simultaneously. We represent the whole system as a directed sensor graph whose edges encode hydraulic connectivity through flow/heat transfer-aware message passing, and we advance the latent dynamics in continuous time via a controlled Neural ODE. A topology-guided missing-node initializer reconstructs uninstrumented states at rollout start; prediction then proceeds fully autoregressively. The GNN-ODE surrogate achieves satisfactory results for the system dynamics prediction. On held-out simulation transients, the surrogate achieves an average MAE of 0.91 K at 60 s and 2.18 K at 300 s for uninstrumented nodes, with $R^2$ up to 0.995 for missing-node state reconstruction. Inference runs at approximately 105 times faster than simulated time on a single GPU, enabling 64-member ensemble rollouts for uncertainty quantification. To assess sim-to-real transfer, we adapt the pretrained surrogate to experimental facility data using layerwise discriminative fine-tuning with only 30 training sequences. The learned flow-dependent heat-transfer scaling recovers a Reynolds-number exponent consistent with established correlations, indicating constitutive learning beyond trajectory fitting. The model tracks a steep power change transient and produces accurate trajectories at uninstrumented locations.

</details>


### [132] [SL-FAC: A Communication-Efficient Split Learning Framework with Frequency-Aware Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07316)
*Zehang Lin,Miao Yang,Haihan Zhu,Zheng Lin,Jianhao Huang,Jing Yang,Guangjin Pan,Dianxin Luan,Zihan Fang,Shunzhi Zhu,Wei Ni,John Thompson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SL-FAC框架，通过自适应频域分解和基于频率的量化压缩技术，有效解决了分割学习中传输 smashed data 的通信瓶颈问题，在大幅降低通信开销的同时保持了模型收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着神经网络复杂度的增加，分割学习（SL）虽然通过模型分区缓解了资源受限设备的计算压力，但参与设备数量和模型复杂度的增加导致 smashed data（如激活值和梯度）传输产生巨大通信开销，成为SL的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出了SL-FAC框架，包含两个核心组件：1）自适应频域分解（AFD），将 smashed data 转换到频域并分解为具有不同信息特征的光谱分量；2）基于频率的量化压缩（FQC），根据各分量的光谱能量分布，为其应用定制化的量化位宽，从而在保留对模型收敛最关键信息的同时实现高效压缩。

Result: 大量实验证实SL-FAC在提升训练效率方面具有优越性能，能够显著减少通信量，同时战略性地保留了模型收敛所需的关键信息。

Conclusion: SL-FAC成功实现了通信高效的分割学习，通过频域处理和差异化量化策略有效平衡了通信开销与模型性能，为资源受限设备上的分布式机器学习部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The growing complexity of neural networks hinders the deployment of distributed machine learning on resource-constrained devices. Split learning (SL) offers a promising solution by partitioning the large model and offloading the primary training workload from edge devices to an edge server. However, the increasing number of participating devices and model complexity leads to significant communication overhead from the transmission of smashed data (e.g., activations and gradients), which constitutes a critical bottleneck for SL. To tackle this challenge, we propose SL-FAC, a communication-efficient SL framework comprising two key components: adaptive frequency decomposition (AFD) and frequency-based quantization compression (FQC). AFD first transforms the smashed data into the frequency domain and decomposes it into spectral components with distinct information. FQC then applies customized quantization bit widths to each component based on its spectral energy distribution. This collaborative approach enables SL-FAC to achieve significant communication reduction while strategically preserving the information most crucial for model convergence. Extensive experiments confirm the superior performance of SL-FAC for improving the training efficiency.

</details>


### [133] [How to sketch a learning algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07328)
*Sam Gunn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够在深度学习环境下以极小误差预测数据删除后模型行为的方案，其预计算和预测算法仅比常规训练和推理慢一个多项式因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解训练数据选择如何影响AI模型是可解释性、隐私保护和基础科学的核心问题。现有的数据删除问题研究难以直接应用于强大的深度学习模型，因此需要一种既能保证预测精度，又与训练高性能模型兼容的解决方案。

Method: 基于作者提出的"稳定性"假设，开发了一种新的数据删除方案。在技术层面，通过计算随机复数方向上的高阶导数来实现算术电路的局部草图，并利用前向模式自动微分技术高效计算这些导数。预计算和预测算法的时间开销分别比常规训练和推理慢poly(1/ε)倍，存储需求为poly(1/ε)个模型。

Result: 在深度学习环境下，该方案能够以极小误差ε预测数据删除后的模型输出。通过在microgpt上的最小实验集验证了稳定性假设的可行性，证明其与训练强大AI模型完全兼容。代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一种高效且精确的数据删除方案，为理解训练数据对AI模型的影响提供了实用工具。所提出的稳定性假设为后续研究提供了新的理论框架，可能对AI可解释性、隐私保护和基础研究产生重要影响。

Abstract: How does the choice of training data influence an AI model? This question is of central importance to interpretability, privacy, and basic science. At its core is the data deletion problem: after a reasonable amount of precomputation, quickly predict how the model would behave in a given situation if a given subset of training data had been excluded from the learning algorithm.
  We present a data deletion scheme capable of predicting model outputs with vanishing error $\varepsilon$ in the deep learning setting. Our precomputation and prediction algorithms are only $\mathrm{poly}(1/\varepsilon)$ factors slower than regular training and inference, respectively. The storage requirements are those of $\mathrm{poly}(1/\varepsilon)$ models.
  Our proof is based on an assumption that we call "stability." In contrast to the assumptions made by prior work, stability appears to be fully compatible with learning powerful AI models. In support of this, we show that stability is satisfied in a minimal set of experiments with microgpt. Our code is available at https://github.com/SamSpo1/microgpt-sketch.
  At a technical level, our work is based on a new method for locally sketching an arithmetic circuit by computing higher-order derivatives in random complex directions. Forward-mode automatic differentiation allows cheap computation of these derivatives.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [ForkKV: Scaling Multi-LoRA Agent Serving via Copy-on-Write Disaggregated KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06370)
*Shao Wang,Rui Ren,Lin Gui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ForkKV通过引入操作系统中的fork与写时复制（CoW）机制，解决了多LoRA智能体工作流中因KV缓存分歧导致的显存瓶颈，实现了高达3倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型的多智能体服务工作流中，LoRA适配器的使用导致不同智能体的Key-Value（KV）缓存发生分歧，使得传统前缀缓存失效，迫使维护冗余的KV缓存，迅速耗尽显存并降低吞吐量。

Method: 提出ForkKV系统，借鉴操作系统的fork与写时复制（CoW）机制，将KV缓存物理解耦为大规模共享组件和轻量级智能体特定组件；设计DualRadixTree架构支持新分叉的智能体继承共享缓存并对独特缓存应用CoW语义；设计ResidualAttention专用内核在片上SRAM内重构解耦的KV缓存。

Result: 在多种语言模型和不同任务数据集上的评估表明，ForkKV的吞吐量相比最先进的多LoRA服务系统提升了多达3.0倍，且对生成质量的影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: ForkKV成功解决了多LoRA智能体工作流中的显存瓶颈问题，通过创新的内存管理机制显著提升了服务吞吐量，为复杂多智能体LLM服务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The serving paradigm of large language models (LLMs) is rapidly shifting towards complex multi-agent workflows where specialized agents collaborate over massive shared contexts. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables the efficient co-hosting of these specialized agents on a single base model, it introduces a critical memory footprint bottleneck during serving. Specifically, unique LoRA activations cause Key-Value (KV) cache divergence across agents, rendering traditional prefix caching ineffective for shared contexts. This forces redundant KV cache maintenance, rapidly saturating GPU capacity and degrading throughput.
  To address this challenge, we introduce ForkKV, a serving system for multi-LoRA agent workflows centered around a novel memory management paradigm in OS: fork with copy-on-write (CoW). By exploiting the structural properties of LoRA, ForkKV physically decouples the KV cache into a massive shared component (analogous to the parent process's memory pages) and lightweight agent-specific components (the child process's pages). To support this mechanism, we propose a DualRadixTree architecture that allows newly forked agents to inherit the massive shared cache and apply CoW semantics for their lightweight unique cache. Furthermore, to guarantee efficient execution, we design ResidualAttention, a specialized kernel that reconstructs the disaggregated KV cache directly within on-chip SRAM. Comprehensive evaluations across diverse language models and practical datasets of different tasks demonstrate that ForkKV achieves up to 3.0x the throughput of state-of-the-art multi-LoRA serving systems with a negligible impact on generation quality.

</details>


### [135] [Contextual Chain: Single-State Ledger Design for Mobile/IoT Networks with Frequent Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06529)
*Song-Ju Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种面向间歇性和噪声网络（如物联网和移动场景）的轻量级账本协议。核心发现是：隔离机制本身在噪声条件下无法显著改善共识，而自适应同步策略能显著提升分区恢复后的最终共识概率和恢复时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物联网和移动环境中，网络分区频繁发生，且完整历史验证在实践中不可行。现有的账本协议在这些间歇性、噪声网络环境下表现不佳，需要设计一种轻量级协议来处理网络分区和重新连接的动态过程。

Method: 设计基于上下文认证的轻量级账本协议，包含三个核心机制：1）检查点优先的分叉选择规则；2）基于近期提议者行为的本地分支评分；3）不一致性驱动的隔离信号。同时引入自适应同步机制，仅在不一致性普遍存在时增加Gossip通信开销。通过离散事件模拟器在受控分区和两种网络模式（清洁和噪声）下进行评估。

Result: 在N=20节点、500个随机种子的实验中，隔离机制单独使用时在噪声条件下无法显著改善共识或恢复性能；而增强同步策略（Gossip_only和Both）显著提升了分区重新连接后的最终共识概率和恢复时间尾部表现。长时程实验表明低同步失败不会随时间自动消除。在N=50和N=100的扩展实验中，小规模环境下的参数无法直接推广到大规模场景。

Conclusion: 在噪声分区/重新连接动态环境下，当前设计的恢复能力主要受信息可用性限制，同步策略应作为一等设计问题对待。这为后续研究指明了方向：需要重点优化信息传播和同步机制以提升网络分区恢复能力。

Abstract: We study a lightweight ledger protocol for intermittent and noisy networks, motivated by IoT and mobile settings in which partitions are common and full-history verification is impractical. Our design centers on an \emph{operational} notion of \textbf{contextual authentication}: each node decides whether a chain extension is acceptable in its current local context, using checkpoint-first fork choice, a local branch score derived from recent proposer behavior, and an inconsistency-driven \emph{quarantine} signal. To improve recovery after partitions, we combine this acceptance rule with \textbf{adaptive synchronization}, which increases gossip effort only when inconsistency becomes prevalent.
  We evaluate the protocol with a discrete-event simulator under controlled partitions and two network regimes (clean and noisy). Across 500 seeds at $N=20$, the main result is that quarantine alone does not materially improve agreement or recovery under noisy conditions, whereas increased synchronization (\texttt{Gossip\_only} and \texttt{Both}) substantially improves both final agreement probability and recovery-time tails after partition rejoin. Longer-horizon experiments show that low-synchronization failures are not removed simply by waiting longer, and scaling experiments at $N=50$ and $N=100$ show that parameters that work at small scale do not automatically generalize. These results indicate that, under noisy partition/rejoin dynamics, recovery in the current design is limited primarily by information availability, making synchronization policy a first-class design problem.

</details>


### [136] [DynLP: Parallel Dynamic Batch Update for Label Propagation in Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06596)
*S M Shovan,Arindam Khanda,S M Ferdous,Sajal K. Das,Mahantesh Halappanavar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出DynLP算法，通过GPU加速实现动态批处理并行标签传播，仅对相关子图进行必要更新，避免全量重计算，相比现有方法最高提速102倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图半监督学习中的标签传播算法在面对增量到达的数据时，需要重新计算所有标签，存在冗余计算量大、效率低下的关键问题。

Method: 提出一种以GPU为中心的动态批处理并行标签传播算法（DynLP），仅执行必要的更新操作，将变化传播至相关子图，无需全量重计算，并充分利用GPU架构进行优化。

Result: 在大规模数据集上，相比最先进的方法，平均实现13倍加速，最高可达102倍加速。

Conclusion: DynLP有效解决了动态增量数据场景下标签传播的效率瓶颈，显著提升了计算性能，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning aims to infer class labels using only a small fraction of labeled data. In graph-based semi-supervised learning, this is typically achieved through label propagation to predict labels of unlabeled nodes. However, in real-world applications, data often arrive incrementally in batches. Each time a new batch appears, reapplying the traditional label propagation algorithm to recompute all labels is redundant, computationally intensive, and inefficient. To address the absence of an efficient label propagation update method, we propose DynLP, a novel GPU-centric Dynamic Batched Parallel Label Propagation algorithm that performs only the necessary updates, propagating changes to the relevant subgraph without requiring full recalculation. By exploiting GPU architectural optimizations, our algorithm achieves on average 13x and upto 102x speedup on large-scale datasets compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [137] [Sparsity-Aware Roofline Models for Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06637)
*Matthew Qian,Yahia Ramadan,Suhita Anubha,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏矩阵-稠密矩阵乘法（SpMM）的性能分析，发现单一的Roofline模型无法准确预测不同稀疏结构下的性能，需要建立稀疏感知的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpMM是科学计算、图分析和机器学习中的关键核心，其性能常受内存带宽限制。现有的Roofline建模方法没有充分考虑矩阵稀疏结构对算术强度和可达性能的影响，因此需要研究Roofline模型在SpMM中的适用性和局限性。

Method: 1. 评估三种SpMM实现：压缩稀疏行（CSR）、压缩稀疏块（CSB）和Intel MKL库；
2. 使用SuiteSparse集合中的大规模矩阵，按稀疏模式分组（块结构、带状/对角、无标度和均匀随机矩阵）；
3. 推导稀疏感知的Roofline模型，综合考虑内存流量、缓存局部性和分块行为；
4. 在AMD架构的Perlmutter计算节点上，改变稠密矩阵列数进行实验测试。

Result: 1. 单一的Roofline模型不足以准确预测不同稀疏结构下的性能；
2. 分块和结构化稀疏性显著改变有效算术强度；
3. 不同稀疏模式（块结构、带状、无标度、随机）对性能影响差异显著；
4. CSR、CSB和MKL三种实现在不同矩阵结构下表现各异。

Conclusion: SpMM的精确Roofline性能分析需要稀疏感知建模方法，数据布局和分块策略必须在矩阵结构的上下文中评估，而不能通过单一统一模型来实现。这为SpMM的性能优化和建模提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Sparse matrix-dense matrix multiplication (SpMM) is a critical kernel in scientific computing, graph analytics, and machine learning, whose performance is often constrained by memory bandwidth. In this work, we investigate the applicability and limitations of roofline modeling for SpMM by explicitly accounting for the impact of matrix sparsity structure on arithmetic intensity and attainable performance. We evaluate three SpMM implementations: Compressed Sparse Row (CSR), Compressed Sparse Blocks (CSB), and Intel's Math Kernel Library (MKL). Each implementation was tested using large-scale matrices from the SuiteSparse collection and grouped by sparsity pattern, including block-structured, banded (diagonal), scale-free, and uniformly random matrices. We derive sparsity-aware roofline models that incorporate memory traffic, cache locality, and blocking behavior, and demonstrate that a single model is insufficient to accurately predict performance across diverse structures. Experiments were conducted on an AMD-based Perlmutter compute node with a varying number of columns in the dense matrix. In particular, blocking and structured sparsity significantly alter effective arithmetic intensity. The results show that accurate roofline-based performance analysis of SpMM requires sparsity-aware modeling, and that data layout and blocking strategies must be evaluated in the context of matrix structure rather than through a single unified model.

</details>


### [138] [Foundry: Template-Based CUDA Graph Context Materialization for Fast LLM Serving Cold Start](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06664)
*Xueshen Liu,Yongji Wu,Yuncheng Yao,Danyang Zhuo,Ion Stoica,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Foundry系统，通过模板化的CUDA图上下文物化技术，将LLM服务的冷启动延迟降低至多99%，解决了CUDA图捕获耗时长的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM服务提供商依赖自动扩缩容和平行性重配置来应对快速变化的工作负载，但冷启动延迟仍是主要瓶颈。虽然现有系统已将模型权重加载缩短至秒级，但CUDA图捕获仍需数十秒到数分钟，且CUDA图与执行上下文紧密耦合，难以简单序列化。

Method: 提出Foundry系统，采用模板化CUDA图上下文物化方法：1）在离线阶段持久化图拓扑和执行上下文；2）强制确定性内存布局；3）自动提取并重载图所需的内核二进制文件；4）通过基于拓扑的模板化降低在线重构成本；5）支持单GPU离线捕获生成多GPU部署模板，仅需修补依赖rank的通信状态。

Result: 在参数量高达235B的密集模型和MoE模型上，Foundry将冷启动延迟降低至多99%。对于Qwen3-235B-A22B模型，初始化时间从10分钟缩短至3.9秒，同时保持CUDA图的吞吐量增益。

Conclusion: Foundry成功解决了CUDA图捕获的序列化难题，实现了近乎即时的冷启动，使得LLM服务能够更灵活地应对动态工作负载变化，同时保留了CUDA图带来的性能优势。

Abstract: Modern LLM service providers increasingly rely on autoscaling and parallelism reconfiguration to respond to rapidly changing workloads, but cold-start latency remains a major bottleneck. While recent systems have reduced model weight loading to seconds, CUDA graph capture still takes tens of seconds to minutes and often dominates startup. Unfortunately, CUDA graphs cannot be naively serialized: beyond graph topology, they are tightly coupled to execution context, including device addresses embedded in kernel arguments and kernel code lazily loaded during warmup. Existing approaches either rely on brittle kernel-specific patching or heavyweight process-level checkpoint/restore that are inflexible to dynamic parallelism switching. We present Foundry, a template-based CUDA graph context materialization system that persists both graph topology and execution context during an offline processing stage, and reconstructs executable graphs online with negligible overhead. Foundry enforces deterministic memory layouts, automatically extracts and reloads kernel binaries required by captured graphs, and reduces online reconstruction costs through topology-based templating. For distributed serving, Foundry further enables a single-GPU offline capture to generate templates for multi-GPU deployments by patching only rank-dependent communication state. Across dense and MoE models up to 235B parameters, Foundry reduces cold-start latency by up to 99%, cutting the initialization time of Qwen3-235B-A22B from 10 minutes to 3.9 seconds while preserving the throughput gains of CUDA graphs.

</details>


### [139] [Nexus: Transparent I/O Offloading for High-Density Serverless Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06682)
*JooYoung Park,Kevin Nguetchouang,Jovan Stojkovic,Likun Zhang,Riccardo Mancini,Marco Cali,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nexus，一种无服务器原生的KVM虚拟机监视器，通过在API边界透明地解耦计算与I/O，将通信架构卸载到主机共享后端，显著降低了资源开销并提升了部署密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无服务计算架构中，应用处理逻辑与I/O处理紧密耦合，导致每个虚拟机都必须复制沉重的通信架构（云SDK、RPC和TCP/IP），占用了超过25%的函数内存占用，并可能导致VM中的CPU周期比裸机执行增加一倍。虽然之前有系统尝试使用WebAssembly或库操作系统来解决此问题，但它们牺牲了生态系统兼容性，迫使开发人员将代码和依赖项迁移到新的语言。

Method: 引入Nexus，一种基于KVM的无服务器原生虚拟机监视器。其核心方法是在API边界拦截通信架构，并通过零拷贝共享内存将其卸载到始终在线的主机共享后端。这种设计将重量级通信架构从客户VM中移除，同时保留了传统的无服务器编程模型。通过结构化分离这些域，Nexus还实现了异步I/O优化：将输入负载预取与VM快照恢复重叠，以及将输出负载写回存储的操作移出关键路径。

Result: 与生产基线相比，Nexus将节点级CPU和内存总消耗分别降低了44%和31%，从而将部署密度提高了37%。同时，Nexus分别将暖启动和冷启动延迟降低了39%和10%，使其响应时间达到与不兼容生态系统的基于WASM的虚拟机监视器相差20%以内的水平。

Conclusion: Nexus成功地在保持生态系统兼容性的前提下，通过透明解耦计算与I/O，显著降低了无服务器计算的资源消耗和启动延迟，提高了部署密度，证明了在VM环境中实现高性能无服务器计算的可行性。

Abstract: Serverless computing relies on extreme multi-tenancy to remain economically viable, driving providers to rely on virtual machines (VMs) that ensure strong isolation and seamless ecosystem compatibility with the FaaS programming model. However, current architectures tightly couple application processing logic with I/O processing, forcing every VM to duplicate a heavy communication fabric (cloud SDK, RPC, and TCP/IP). Our analysis reveals this duplication consumes over 25% of a function's memory footprint, and may double the CPU cycles in VMs compared to bare-metal execution. While prior systems attempt to solve this using WebAssembly or library OSes, they naively sacrifice ecosystem compatibility, forcing developers to migrate code and dependencies to new languages.
  We introduce Nexus, a serverless-native KVM-based hypervisor that transparently decouples compute from I/O. Nexus shifts the execution model by intercepting communication fabric at the API boundary and offloading it to an always-on host shared backend via zero-copy shared memory. This removes the heavyweight communication fabric from the guest VM, while preserving the conventional serverless programming model. By structurally separating these domains, Nexus unlocks asynchronous I/O optimizations: overlapping input payload prefetching with VM restoration from a snapshot and writing output payloads back to storage off the critical path. Compared to the production baseline, Nexus reduces overall node-level CPU and memory consumption by up to 44% and 31%, respectively, thus increasing deployment density by 37%. Also, Nexus reduces warm- and cold-start latency by 39% and 10%, respectively, bringing the response time within 20% of that of a WASM-based, ecosystem-incompatible hypervisor.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond End-to-End: Dynamic Chain Optimization for Private LLM Adaptation on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06819)
*Yebo Wu,Jingguang Li,Chunlin Tian,Kahou Tam,Zhijiang Guo,Li Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出ChainFed方法，通过逐层顺序训练适配器的方式解决联邦微调大语言模型时的内存瓶颈问题，性能提升高达46.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调大语言模型面临关键瓶颈：大语言模型的高内存需求与边缘设备有限容量之间的差异，传统端到端更新方式在资源受限场景下难以实施。

Method: 提出链式联邦微调（ChainFed）范式，放弃端到端更新，采用顺序逐层训练方式。先训练初始适配器至收敛并冻结权重，再进行下一层训练，形成优化链。集成三项核心技术：1）动态层协同调优，弥补顺序调优层间的语义差距；2）全局感知优化，使每个适配器具备超越局部目标的前瞻能力；3）功能导向自适应调优，自动识别最优微调起点。

Result: 在多个基准测试上进行广泛实验，ChainFed相比现有方法表现优异，平均准确率提升高达46.46%。

Conclusion: ChainFed成功突破联邦微调大语言模型的内存障碍，为边缘设备上的隐私保护LLM适应提供了一种有效的解决方案，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning enables privacy-preserving LLM adaptation but faces a critical bottleneck: the disparity between LLMs' high memory demands and edge devices' limited capacity. To break the memory barrier, we propose Chain Federated Fine-Tuning (ChainFed), an innovative paradigm that forgoes end-to-end updates in favor of a sequential, layer-by-layer manner. It first trains the initial adapter to convergence, freezes its weights, and then proceeds to the next. This iterative train-and-freeze process forms an optimization chain, gradually enhancing the model's task-specific proficiency. ChainFed further integrates three core techniques: 1) Dynamic Layer Co-Tuning to bridge semantic gaps between sequentially tuned layers and facilitate information flow; 2) Globally Perceptive Optimization to endow each adapter with foresight beyond its local objective; 3) Function-Oriented Adaptive Tuning to automatically identify the optimal fine-tuning starting point. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of ChainFed over existing methods, boosting average accuracy by up to 46.46\%.

</details>


### [141] [Exploiting Aggregate Programming in a Multi-Robot Service Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06876)
*Giorgio Audrito,Andrea Basso,Daniele Bortoluzzi,Ferruccio Damiani,Giordano Scarso,Gianluca Torta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个采用聚合编程（AP）来设计和实现多机器人服务系统协调软件的原型，并通过仿真和大学图书馆实地测试进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在医疗、探索和救援等领域应用日益广泛，但由于机器人及其环境的物理特性与分布式系统协调的复杂性叠加，构建此类系统仍面临重大挑战。聚合编程（AP）作为近期兴起的一种工程化弹性分布式系统的方法，为解决上述问题提供了新的可能性。

Method: 设计并实现了一个多机器人服务系统原型，其核心在于采用聚合编程（AP）方法来开发协调软件。通过基于邻近性的通信机制来实现分布式协调。研究团队使用仿真测试和大学图书馆实地测试两种方式对原型进行了验证。

Result: 成功构建了一个基于聚合编程的多机器人服务系统原型，该原型在仿真环境和真实的大学图书馆场景中均通过了验证测试。

Conclusion: 聚合编程方法能够有效应用于多机器人系统协调软件的设计与实现，为解决多机器人系统构建中的复杂性挑战提供了一种可行的工程化解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems are becoming increasingly relevant within diverse application domains, such as healthcare, exploration, and rescue missions. However, building such systems is still a significant challenge, since it adds the complexities of the physical nature of robots and their environments to those inherent in coordinating any distributed (multi-agent) system. Aggregate Programming (AP) has recently emerged as a promising approach to engineering resilient, distributed systems with proximity-based communication, and is notably supported by practical frameworks.  In this paper we present a prototype of a multi-robot service system, which adopts AP for the design and implementation of its coordination software. The prototype has been validated both with simulations, and with tests in a University library.

</details>


### [142] [On the Decidability of Distributed Tasks with Output Sets under Asynchrony and Any Number of Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06920)
*Timothé Albouy,Antonio Fernández Anta,Chryssis Georgiou,Nicolas Nicolaou,Junlang Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文定义了SOS任务（输出集合的集合任务），证明了该类任务在t个崩溃下的异步可解性是可判定的：当t=0时总是可解，当t>0时当且仅当其SOS图（顶点为输出集合，边表示包含关系）连通时才可解。此结果揭示了无有效性条件下k-集协议的可解性规律，并提出了d-分歧任务，其实现条件与调和级数相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算中存在一类任务，其输出由可能的输出值集合的集合定义。作者希望定义这一类任务并研究其异步可解性的判定问题，即是否存在有效过程能够判断任意此类任务在t个崩溃下是否可解。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了SOS任务，其由可产生的不同输出值集合的集合O刻画。然后引入SOS图G=(O,⊂)，其中每个顶点是一个输出集合，两个顶点之间存在边当且仅当一个输出集合包含另一个。作者通过图论方法分析SOS图的连通性与任务可解性之间的关系，建立了判定规则。

Result: 证明了SOS任务的可解性是可判定的：(1)当t=0时，所有SOS任务均可解；(2)当t>0时，SOS任务可解当且仅当其SOS图连通。由此推导出一个令人惊讶的结论：无有效性条件下，当k>1时k-集协议在任意t≥0个崩溃下均可解，仅当k=1（共识）时在t>0个崩溃下不可解。此外，研究了d-分歧任务并发现其实现条件与调和级数相关。

Conclusion: 本文成功地为SOS任务建立了完整的可解性判定理论，揭示了图连通性在分布式任务可解性中的核心作用。研究结果深化了对k-集协议和共识问题本质的理解，并为设计新的分布式任务提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we define a new class of distributed tasks, called SOS tasks (for Set of Output Sets tasks), defined by the set $O$ of distinct output sets of values that can be produced. We then demonstrate that this class of tasks is decidable: there exists an effective procedure that determines whether any SOS task is solvable asynchronously under $t$ crashes. The decision rule is as follows. Every SOS task is solvable when $t=0$. For $t > 0$, an SOS task is solvable if and only if its SOS graph $G=(O,\subset)$ is connected. In this graph, each vertex is an output set in $O$, and two vertices are linked by an edge whenever one output set includes the other. One of the surprising implications of our results is that, without a validity property, $k$-set agreement is solvable under any number of crashes $t \geq 0$ for $k>1$, and unsolvable under $t >0$ crashes only for $k=1$ (consensus). Finally, we study a novel family of tasks called $d$-disagreement, which requires the system to always produce $d$ different output values, and we show that its implementability condition is related to the harmonic series.

</details>


### [143] [NestPipe: Large-Scale Recommendation Training on 1,500+ Accelerators via Nested Pipelining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06956)
*Zhida Jiang,Zhaolong Xing,Huichao Chai,Tianxing Sun,Qiang Peng,Baopeng Yuan,Jiaxing Wang,Hua Du,Zhixin Wu,Xuemiao Li,Yikui Cao,Xinyu Liu,Yongxiang Feng,Zhen Chen,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了NestPipe，一个大规模去中心化Embedding训练框架，通过双层流水线技术同时解决了大规模推荐模型训练中的查找和通信瓶颈，在保持同步训练语义的前提下实现了高达3.06倍的加速和94.07%的扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型参数量已达万亿级别，当集群规模扩展至千卡量级时，分布式训练的瓶颈从计算和内存转移到了数据移动，特别是与Embedding相关的查找延迟和通信延迟。现有方案要么仅优化单一瓶颈，要么通过牺牲训练一致性来提升吞吐量，无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出了NestPipe框架，利用嵌套流水线实现两层稀疏并行：(1) 批次间层面，提出双缓冲流水线（DBP），通过双缓冲同步构建无陈旧度的五阶段流水线，在不引入Embedding陈旧度的情况下缓解查找瓶颈；(2) 批次内层面，发现Embedding冻结现象，提出冻结窗口流水线（FWP），通过协调流调度和以键为中心的样本聚类，实现All2All通信与密集计算的重叠。

Result: 在包含1,536个工作节点的生产环境GPU和NPU集群上的实验表明，NestPipe实现了最高3.06倍的加速比和94.07%的扩展效率，验证了其在解决大规模推荐模型训练瓶颈方面的有效性。

Conclusion: NestPipe成功实现了在保持同步训练语义的前提下同时优化查找和通信瓶颈，证明了双层嵌套流水线设计在大规模Embedding训练中的优越性，为大规模推荐模型的高效训练提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern recommendation models have increased to trillions of parameters. As cluster scales expand to O(1k), distributed training bottlenecks shift from computation and memory to data movement, especially lookup and communication latency associated with embeddings. Existing solutions either optimize only one bottleneck or improve throughput by sacrificing training consistency. This paper presents NestPipe, a large-scale decentralized embedding training framework that tackles both bottlenecks while preserving synchronous training semantics. NestPipe exploits two hierarchical sparse parallelism opportunities through nested pipelining. At the inter-batch level, Dual-Buffer Pipelining (DBP) constructs a staleness-free five-stage pipeline through dual-buffer synchronization, mitigating lookup bottlenecks without embedding staleness. At the intra-batch level, we identify the embedding freezing phenomenon, which inspires Frozen-Window Pipelining (FWP) to overlap All2All communication with dense computation via coordinated stream scheduling and key-centric sample clustering. Experiments on production GPU and NPU clusters with 1,536 workers demonstrate that NestPipe achieves up to 3.06x speedup and 94.07% scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [144] [Scheduling the Unschedulable: Taming Black-Box LLM Inference at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06970)
*Renzhong Yuan,Yijun Zeng,Xiaosong Gao,Linxi Yu,Haochun Liao,Han Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对黑盒LLM API的客户端调度方法，利用可预测的输出token数量实现半预知调度，通过三层分解架构在保证服务质量的同时显著提升系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在输出token数量可在提交时预测的情况下，客户端对黑盒LLM API的调度变得半预知化：决策可以基于粗粒度的token先验信息，即使服务提供商的内部机制仍然不可见。这为客户端调度提供了新的优化机会。

Method: 将边界问题分解为三个可分离的关注点：(1)分配层：通过自适应DRR实现类间共享；(2)排序层：使用可行集评分进行类内排序；(3)过载控制：在成本阶梯上进行明确的接受/延迟/拒绝决策。

Result: 在平衡/高拥塞场景下实现100%完成率、100%截止时间满足率，SLO满足的有效吞吐量达4.2±1.6请求/秒，短请求P95延迟在数十毫秒内。粗粒度幅度先验是实用的控制阈值，去除幅度信息会使短请求P95增加5.8倍。在60%倍数误差下仍保持优雅降级。公平排队相比FIFO在短请求P90提升32%，长请求开销仅增加17%。

Conclusion: 三层客户端分解架构能够有效适应不同的公平性目标而无需改变其余堆栈，成本阶梯丢弃策略与声明的服务目标相关联，为客户端调度提供了可控的评估框架。

Abstract: When output token counts can be predicted at submission time (Gan et al., 2026), client-side scheduling against a black-box LLM API becomes semi-clairvoyant: decisions condition on coarse token priors even though the provider's internals remain hidden. We decompose this boundary problem into three separable concerns: allocation (inter-class share via adaptive DRR), ordering (intra-class sequencing with feasible-set scoring), and overload control (explicit admit/defer/reject on a cost ladder). An information ladder experiment shows that coarse magnitude priors -- not class labels alone -- are the practical threshold for useful client control; removing magnitude inflates short-request P95 by up to $5.8\times$ and degrades deadline satisfaction. Under balanced / high congestion the full stack achieves 100% completion, 100% deadline satisfaction, and useful goodput of $4.2 \pm 1.6$ SLO-meeting requests/s with short P95 within tens of milliseconds of quota-tiered isolation. A predictor-noise sweep confirms graceful degradation under up to 60% multiplicative error. Heavy-dominated regimes separate policies on completion, tail, and interpretable shedding. We further compare short-priority allocation (biased toward interactive traffic) with Fair Queuing (round-robin across classes): Fair Queuing achieves +32% short-request P90 improvement over FIFO with only +17% long-request overhead, versus Short-Priority's +27% / +116% trade-off -- demonstrating that the allocation layer accommodates different fairness objectives without changing the remaining stack. We contribute the three-layer client-side decomposition, controlled evaluation of joint metrics across regimes, allocation-policy alternatives, and overload-policy evidence linking cost-ladder shedding to the stated service objective.

</details>


### [145] [Autopoiesis: A Self-Evolving System Paradigm for LLM Serving Under Runtime Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07144)
*Youhe Jiang,Ran Yan,You Peng,Wenshuang Li,Taiyi Wang,Fangcheng Fu,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Autopoiesis系统，利用LLM驱动的程序合成在线持续演化服务策略，将策略设计从一次性离线工作转变为持续的系统组件，相比现有系统平均提升34%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM服务环境具有高度波动性，存在工作负载波动和集群弹性伸缩等运行时动态。传统静态人工设计的服务策略难以适应运行时权衡（如调度开销与执行效率、重调度频率与重配置开销）的持续变化，无法实现最优平衡。

Method: 提出了Autopoiesis在线自演化系统：（1）引入LLM驱动的程序合成工作流，根据实时观察的动态演化服务策略，使演化策略反映在复杂多维权衡空间中的最优决策；（2）使合成过程在服务期间持续运行，观察实际系统行为并在运行时权衡变化时重写策略代码，将策略设计转变为持续的系统组件。

Result: 在多种运行时动态环境下评估Autopoiesis，相比最先进的LLM服务系统实现了最高53%、平均34%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 建立了新范式：服务策略不再是部署前由人工设计的静态产物，而是LLM在部署过程中持续演化的活代码，能够驾驭超越人类设计能力的运行时权衡。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Model (LLM) serving operates in highly volatile environments characterized by severe runtime dynamics, such as workload fluctuations and elastic cluster autoscaling. Traditional serving systems rely on static, human-engineered serving policies (e.g., scheduling algorithms and rescheduling strategies) to manage these dynamics. However, these policies must navigate deeply intertwined runtime trade-offs (e.g., scheduling overhead vs. execution efficiency, rescheduling frequency vs. reconfiguration overhead), whose optimal balance is workload-specific and shifts continuously as runtime conditions evolve, rendering any fixed policy fundamentally unable to adapt. We propose Autopoiesis, a novel online self-evolving system that shifts LLM serving from static policy deployment to continuous online policy evolution. First, Autopoiesis introduces an LLM-driven program synthesis workflow to evolve serving policies with respect to real-time observed dynamics, where the evolved policies reflect the optimal decision in navigating the complex, multi-dimensional trade-off space. Second, Autopoiesis enables this synthesis process to operate continuously during serving, observing real-world system behavior, and rewriting the policy code as runtime trade-offs shift, thereby transforming policy design from a one-time offline endeavor into an ongoing system component, enabling autonomous adaptation to evolving runtime conditions. Together, we establish a new paradigm: Serving policies are no longer static artifacts designed by humans before deployment, but living code that LLMs continuously evolve throughout deployment to navigate runtime trade-offs beyond human design. We evaluate Autopoiesis across diverse runtime dynamics and show up to 53% and on average 34% improvements over state-of-the-art LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [146] [InfiniLoRA: Disaggregated Multi-LoRA Serving for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07173)
*Hongyu Chen,Letian Ruan,Zilin Xu,Yuchen Li,Xinyu Chen,Jingwen Leng,Bingsheng He,Minyi Guo,Shixuan Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了InfiniLoRA，一种解耦的LoRA服务系统，通过将LoRA执行与基础模型推理分离，解决了MoE架构下LoRA内存成本高的问题，在严格延迟SLO下实现了3.05倍的服务请求率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA服务设计采用耦合架构，在新兴的MoE模型架构下显著增加了LoRA内存成本，导致系统可扩展性差且容易出现尾部延迟膨胀问题，难以满足多租户和多任务服务需求。

Method: 提出InfiniLoRA解耦LoRA服务系统，引入共享LoRA服务器并实现：1）感知并行性的执行策略；2）SLO驱动的资源供给；3）关键路径优化技术，包括GPU发起的通信机制和硬件专用LoRA内核。

Result: 实验结果表明，InfiniLoRA在严格延迟SLO约束下平均服务请求率提升3.05倍，满足SLO要求的LoRA适配器比例提高54.0%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦架构设计和系统级优化，InfiniLoRA有效解决了MoE架构下LoRA服务的可扩展性和延迟问题，为大规模多租户多任务LLM服务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: LoRA enables efficient customization of LLMs and is widely used in multi-tenant and multi-task serving. However, emerging model architectures such as MoE significantly increase LoRA memory cost, making existing coupled LoRA serving designs poorly scalable and prone to tail-latency inflation. We present InfiniLoRA, a disaggregated LoRA serving system that decouples LoRA execution from base-model inference. InfiniLoRA introduces a shared LoRA Server with parallelism-aware execution, SLO-driven provisioning, and critical-path optimizations, including GPU-initiated communication and hardware-specialized LoRA kernels. Experiments show that InfiniLoRA can achieve an average $3.05\times$ increase in serviceable request rate under strict latency SLOs, and improve the percentage of LoRA adapters satisfying the SLO requirement by 54.0\%.

</details>


### [147] [Making Room for AI: Multi-GPU Molecular Dynamics with Deep Potentials in GROMACS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07276)
*Luca Pennati,Andong Hu,Ivy Peng,Lukas Müllender,Stefano Markidis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该工作将机器学习原子间势框架DeePMD-kit集成到GROMACS中，实现了支持多GPU、多节点的神经网络势函数分子动力学模拟，并在32个设备上验证了良好的强/弱扩展性，证明了大规模、近从头算精度的生产级分子动力学模拟是可行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着能以分子动力学吞吐量实现近量子精度的AI驱动原子间势（MLIP）的兴起，如何在多GPU模拟中嵌入神经网络推理并保持高性能，成为经典分子动力学标准软件GROMACS面临的一项重大挑战。

Method: 作者通过扩展GROMACS的NNPot接口以DeePMD作为后端，引入了一个与主模拟解耦的域分解层。推理在所有进程上并发执行，每个时间步使用两次MPI集合通信来广播坐标以及聚合与重分布力。研究使用训练于溶剂化蛋白质片段数据集的DPA-1模型进行验证，并在NVIDIA A100和AMD MI250x GPU上进行了扩展性测试。

Result: 在包含15,668个原子的蛋白质体系上，强扩展效率在16设备时达66%，在32设备时达40%；弱扩展效率在16设备时为80%，在32设备时（MI250x）达48%、（A100）达40%。性能分析显示超过90%的运行时间用于DeePMD推理，MPI通信开销低于10%。主要瓶颈被确认为由截断半径决定的不可避免的鬼原子开销以及进程间的负载不平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了GROMACS与DeePMD的高性能集成，证明了在使用神经网络势函数的情况下，GROMACS能够支持大规模、具有近从头算精度的生产级分子动力学模拟。

Abstract: GROMACS is a de-facto standard for classical Molecular Dynamics (MD). The rise of AI-driven interatomic potentials that pursue near-quantum accuracy at MD throughput now poses a significant challenge: embedding neural-network inference into multi-GPU simulations retaining high-performance. In this work, we integrate the MLIP framework DeePMD-kit into GROMACS, enabling domain-decomposed, GPU-accelerated inference across multi-node systems. We extend the GROMACS NNPot interface with a DeePMD backend, and we introduce a domain decomposition layer decoupled from the main simulation. The inference is executed concurrently on all processes, with two MPI collectives used each step to broadcast coordinates and to aggregate and redistribute forces. We train an in-house DPA-1 model (1.6 M parameters) on a dataset of solvated protein fragments. We validate the implementation on a small protein system, then we benchmark the GROMACS-DeePMD integration with a 15,668 atom protein on NVIDIA A100 and AMD MI250x GPUs up to 32 devices. Strong-scaling efficiency reaches 66% at 16 devices and 40% at 32; weak-scaling efficiency is 80% to 16 devices and reaches 48% (MI250x) and 40% (A100) at 32 devices. Profiling with the ROCm System profiler shows that >90% of the wall time is spent in DeePMD inference, while MPI collectives contribute <10%, primarily since they act as a global synchronization point. The principal bottlenecks are the irreducible ghost-atom cost set by the cutoff radius, confirmed by a simple throughput model, and load imbalance across ranks. These results demonstrate that production MD with near ab initio fidelity is feasible at scale in GROMACS.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Logical Robots: Declarative Multi-Agent Programming in Logica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06629)
*Evgeny Skvortsov,Yilin Xia,Ojaswa Garg,Shawn Bowers,Bertram Ludäscher*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Logical Robots，一个交互式多智能体仿真平台，通过声明式逻辑编程语言Logica定义自主机器人行为，实现了低层反应控制与高层规划的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人编程框架通常将低层控制与高层规划分离，缺乏统一的编程环境，这限制了多智能体机器人行为的探索和开发。作者希望提供一个能够同时处理低层反应控制和高层次规划的统一框架。

Method: 开发了基于逻辑编程语言Logica的交互式多智能体仿真平台。在该平台中，机器人行为通过逻辑谓词声明式定义，这些逻辑谓词将来自模拟雷达阵列和共享内存的观测数据映射到期望的电机输出。

Result: 成功构建了Logical Robots平台，实现了在单一编程环境中同时支持低层反应控制和高层规划的能力，为多智能体机器人行为研究提供了连贯的框架。

Conclusion: 声明式逻辑编程方法能够有效地统一机器人行为的不同抽象层次，为多智能体机器人系统的开发和探索提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: We present Logical Robots, an interactive multi-agent simulation platform where autonomous robot behavior is specified declaratively in the logic programming language Logica. Robot behavior is defined by logical predicates that map observations from simulated radar arrays and shared memory to desired motor outputs. This approach allows low-level reactive control and high-level planning to coexist within a single programming environment, providing a coherent framework for exploring multi-agent robot behavior.

</details>


### [149] [Event-Triggered Adaptive Consensus for Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06813)
*Fidel Aznar,Mar Pujol,Álvaro Díez*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种事件触发的异构机器人集群组织框架，通过自适应共识机制和行为树实现了高效的任务分配和强鲁棒性，在大幅降低通信开销的同时保持了优异的任务完成率和故障恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态变化且通信受限的环境中协调机器人集群是群体智能面临的核心挑战。现有的协调策略往往存在通信开销大、适应性差或对故障鲁棒性不足的问题，亟需一种既能高效完成任务分配，又能自适应环境变化并具备容错能力的新型协调框架。

Method: 提出了一种基于事件触发的组织框架。该方法包含三个核心组件：(1)自适应共识机制，仅在发生重要事件时才启动任务协商通信，消除不必要的交互；(2)自调节协调节奏机制，根据环境冲突水平动态调整协调频率；(3)基于行为树的鲁棒执行模型，管理个体智能体的韧性。整个架构实现了通信效率与任务执行效果的最优平衡。
