Result: 通过大规模仿真实验，将所提方法与五种基准策略进行了对比：无通信反应式行为、简单信息共享协议、基准CBBA算法、周期性CBBA变体以及最先进的C-CBBA算法。实验结果表明，该方法相比通信密集型策略显著降低了网络开销，同时在任务完成数量方面保持顶级性能。此外，该框架对动作执行失败和永久性智能体故障均表现出显著的韧性，验证了其高效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的事件触发框架成功解决了机器人集群在复杂场景下的协调难题，实现了通信效率、任务效能和系统韧性的三重优化。该研究为设计适应性更强、资源效率更高的机器人集群提供了新思路，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Coordinating robotic swarms in dynamic and communication-constrained environments remains a fundamental challenge for collective intelligence. This paper presents a novel framework for event-triggered organization, designed to achieve highly efficient and adaptive task allocation in a heterogeneous robotic swarm. Our approach is based on an adaptive consensus mechanism where communication for task negotiation is initiated only in response to significant events, eliminating unnecessary interactions. Furthermore, the swarm self-regulates its coordination pace based on the level of environmental conflict, and individual agent resilience is managed through a robust execution model based on Behavior Trees. This integrated architecture results in a collective system that is not only effective but also remarkably efficient and adaptive. We validate our framework through extensive simulations, benchmarking its performance against a range of coordination strategies. These include a non-communicating reactive behavior, a simple information-sharing protocol, the baseline Consensus-Based Bundle Algorithm (CBBA), and a periodic CBBA variant integrated within a Behavior Tree architecture. Furthermore, our approach is compared with Clustering-CBBA (C-CBBA), a state-of-the-art algorithm recognized for communication-efficient task management in heterogeneous clusters. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces network overhead when compared to communication-heavy strategies. Moreover, it maintains top-tier mission effectiveness regarding the number of tasks completed, showcasing high efficiency and practicality. The framework also exhibits significant resilience to both action execution and permanent agent failures, highlighting the effectiveness of our event-triggered model for designing adaptive and resource-efficient robotic swarms for complex scenarios.

</details>


### [150] [Generating Local Shields for Decentralised Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06873)
*Haoran Yang,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于进程代数的屏蔽方法，用于在部分可观测的多智能体系统中生成局部安全动作过滤器，有效解决了现有方法依赖全局状态或无记忆过滤器的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的多智能体系统中，智能体的局部动作选择无法确定最终的联合动作，这给安全性维护带来了挑战。现有的屏蔽技术要么假设拥有全局状态访问权限，要么使用无法考虑交互历史的无记忆局部过滤器，存在明显局限性。

Method: 作者引入了一种带有受控选择和递归的屏蔽进程代数，用于在无通信的Dec-POMDP环境中规范安全全局行为。具体流程为：从屏蔽进程编译出进程自动机，进而构建全局Mealy机作为安全联合动作过滤器，最后将其投影到局部Mealy机。这些局部机的状态是与每个智能体观测一致的信念式全局状态子集，输出每个智能体的安全动作集。该方法在Rust中实现并集成了PRISM概率符号模型检测器。

Result: 通过多智能体路径规划案例研究，不同的屏蔽进程相比无屏蔽基线显著减少了碰撞次数，同时展现出不同级别的表达能力和保守性。该方法能够独立于智能体策略计算最佳和最坏情况下的安全概率。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了从全局安全规范到局部过滤器的自动编译，为部分可观测多智能体系统提供了一种有效的安全保障方案，无需全局状态访问或智能体间通信。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems under partial observation often struggle to maintain safety because each agent's locally chosen action does not, in general, determine the resulting joint action. Shielding addresses this by filtering actions based on the current state, but most existing techniques either assume access to a shared centralised global state or employ memoryless local filters that cannot consider interaction history.
  We introduce a shield process algebra with guarded choice and recursion for specifying safe global behaviour in communication-free Dec-POMDP settings. From a shield process, we compile a process automaton, then a global Mealy machine as a safe joint-action filter, and finally project it to local Mealy machines whose states are belief-style subsets of the global Mealy machine states consistent with each agent's observations, and which output per-agent safe action sets.
  We implement the pipeline in Rust and integrate PRISM, the Probabilistic Symbolic Model Checker, to compute best- and worst-case safety probabilities independently of the agents' policies. A multi-agent path-finding case study demonstrates how different shield processes substantially reduce collisions compared to the unshielded baseline while exhibiting varying levels of expressiveness and conservatism.

</details>


### [151] [AgentCity: Constitutional Governance for Autonomous Agent Economies via Separation of Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07007)
*Anbang Ruan,Xing Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出“权力分立（SoP）”治理架构，通过在公链上部署三级智能合约层级，将立法权（智能合约）、执行权（确定性软件）与司法权（人类问责链）分离，以破解多主体智能体协作中的“逻辑垄断”问题。在AgentCity平台进行的50-1000智能体规模预注册实验表明，该架构能够通过问责链对齐实现集体行为的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI智能体跨越组织边界在开放互联网中大规模协作，出现了“逻辑垄断”问题：当不同人类委托人的智能体协作时，集体行为变得不透明，没有任何单一人类能够观察、审计或治理这种涌现行为。这种从规划到执行再到评估的完整逻辑链的垄断，威胁到人类对AI系统的有效控制。

Method: 提出“权力分立（SoP）”宪法式治理架构，在EVM兼容的L2区块链上实现三级智能合约层级（基础层、元层、操作层）。该架构实现三个结构性分离：智能体将操作规则制定为智能合约（立法权）、确定性软件在合约内执行（执行权）、人类通过完整的所有权链进行裁决（司法权），每个智能体都绑定到负责任的人类委托人。

Result: 在AgentCity平台实现了SoP架构的原型系统，并在共享有限资源池、协作产生价值的公共品生产经济场景中进行了预注册实验，实验规模覆盖50到1000个智能体，验证了“通过对齐问责链实现集体行为对齐”的核心论点。

Conclusion: 权力分立架构能够有效破解智能体社会中的逻辑垄断，通过问责链实现的“对齐-通过-问责”机制，可以在不需要自上而下规则的情况下，使智能体集体行为与人类意图对齐，为大规模多主体AI系统的治理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents are beginning to operate across organizational boundaries on the open internet -- discovering, transacting with, and delegating to agents owned by other parties without centralized oversight. When agents from different human principals collaborate at scale, the collective becomes opaque: no single human can observe, audit, or govern the emergent behavior. We term this the Logic Monopoly -- the agent society's unchecked monopoly over the entire logic chain from planning through execution to evaluation. We propose the Separation of Power (SoP) model, a constitutional governance architecture deployed on public blockchain that breaks this monopoly through three structural separations: agents legislate operational rules as smart contracts, deterministic software executes within those contracts, and humans adjudicate through a complete ownership chain binding every agent to a responsible principal. In this architecture, smart contracts are the law itself -- the actual legislative output that agents produce and that governs their behavior. We instantiate SoP in AgentCity on an EVM-compatible layer-2 blockchain (L2) with a three-tier contract hierarchy (foundational, meta, and operational). The core thesis is alignment-through-accountability: if each agent is aligned with its human owner through the accountability chain, then the collective converges on behavior aligned with human intent -- without top-down rules. A pre-registered experiment evaluates this thesis in a commons production economy -- where agents share a finite resource pool and collaboratively produce value -- at 50-1,000 agent scale.

</details>


### [152] [Strategic Persuasion with Trait-Conditioned Multi-Agent Systems for Iterative Legal Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07028)
*Philipp D. Siedler*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了战略法庭框架（Strategic Courtroom Framework），通过多智能体模拟研究法律辩论中的语言劝说机制。研究发现异质性团队表现更优，并开发了基于强化学习的特质编排器，能够动态生成优于人工设计的策略组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有博弈论模型在研究法律、外交和谈判等对抗性领域时，通常忽略了通过语言进行劝说的机制。由于战略互动是通过语言媒介进行的，因此需要研究语言作为战略行动空间的作用机制。

Method: 提出战略法庭框架，这是一个多智能体模拟环境。控辩双方团队由特质条件化的大语言模型（LLM）智能体组成，进行迭代式的法庭辩论。智能体通过九个可解释特质和四种原型进行实例化，实现对修辞风格和战略导向的系统控制。使用DeepSeek-R1和Gemini 2.5 Pro在10个合成法律案例和84种三特质团队配置上进行超过7000次模拟试验。同时开发了基于强化学习的特质编排器（Trait Orchestrator），动态生成防御策略。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）具有互补特质的异质性团队持续优于同质性配置；（2）适度的交互深度产生更稳定的判决结果；（3）某些特质（特别是量化型和魅力型）对劝说成功贡献显著；（4）基于强化学习的特质编排器发现的策略优于静态的、人工设计的特质组合。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了如何将语言作为一等战略行动空间来处理，为构建能够在多智能体环境中进行自适应劝说的自主智能体提供了基础，推动了语言驱动的战略互动研究。

Abstract: Strategic interaction in adversarial domains such as law, diplomacy, and negotiation is mediated by language, yet most game-theoretic models abstract away the mechanisms of persuasion that operate through discourse. We present the Strategic Courtroom Framework, a multi-agent simulation environment in which prosecution and defense teams composed of trait-conditioned Large Language Model (LLM) agents engage in iterative, round-based legal argumentation. Agents are instantiated using nine interpretable traits organized into four archetypes, enabling systematic control over rhetorical style and strategic orientation.
  We evaluate the framework across 10 synthetic legal cases and 84 three-trait team configurations, totaling over 7{,}000 simulated trials using DeepSeek-R1 and Gemini~2.5~Pro. Our results show that heterogeneous teams with complementary traits consistently outperform homogeneous configurations, that moderate interaction depth yields more stable verdicts, and that certain traits (notably quantitative and charismatic) contribute disproportionately to persuasive success. We further introduce a reinforcement-learning-based Trait Orchestrator that dynamically generates defense traits conditioned on the case and opposing team, discovering strategies that outperform static, human-designed trait combinations.
  Together, these findings demonstrate how language can be treated as a first-class strategic action space and provide a foundation for building autonomous agents capable of adaptive persuasion in multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [153] [Designing for Accountable Agents: a Viewpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07204)
*Stephen Cranefield,Nir Oren*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对AI系统中问责制概念模糊的问题，在多智能体系统（MAS）层面提出了问责制的定义框架，通过跨学科调研明确了概念，并指出了构建问责智能体的研究挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统日益复杂、普及和自主化，人们对其社会影响日益担忧。虽然AI决策的透明性、可解释性和伦理符合性已受到关注，但问责制这一关键概念仍难以定义。现有研究多关注人类组织层面的问责，而忽视了多智能体系统中智能体本身的问责需求，因此需要在MAS层面明确和实现问责制。

Method: （1）对多个学科中关于问责制的现有工作进行深入调研，寻求该概念的连贯定义；（2）给出一个多智能体系统应用领域的现实案例，说明使智能体遵循问责流程的益处；（3）识别MAS社区在构建问责智能体时面临的一系列研究挑战，并概述初步解决方案。

Result: 建立了问责制的跨学科定义框架；通过现实案例展示了问责智能体在开放社会技术系统中的价值；识别并列举了构建问责智能体的关键研究挑战，提供了初步解决思路。

Conclusion: 本文为在开放社会技术系统中实现自主元素的问责流程奠定了基础，为MAS社区提供了构建问责智能体的研究路线图，指明了未来研究的方向和重点。

Abstract: AI systems are becoming increasingly complex, ubiquitous and autonomous, leading to increasing concerns about their impacts on individuals and society. In response, researchers have begun investigating how to ensure that the methods underlying AI decision-making are transparent and their decisions are explainable to people and conformant to human values and ethical principles. As part of this research thrust, the need for accountability within AI systems has been noted, but this notion has proven elusive to define; we aim to address this issue in the current paper. Unlike much recent work, we do not address accountability within the human organisational processes of developing and deploying AI; rather we consider what it would it mean for the agents within a multi-agent system (MAS), potentially including human agents, to be accountable to other agents or to have others accountable to them.
  In this work, we make the following contributions: we provide an in-depth survey of existing work on accountability in multiple disciplines, seeking to identify a coherent definition of the concept; we give a realistic example of a multi-agent system application domain that illustrates the benefits of enabling agents to follow accountability processes, and we identify a set of research challenges for the MAS community in building accountable agents, sketching out some initial solutions to these, thereby laying out a road-map for future research. Our focus is on laying the groundwork to enable autonomous elements within open socio-technical systems to take part in accountability processes.

</details>


### [154] [Intertemporal Demand Allocation for Inventory Control in Online Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07312)
*Rene Caldentey,Tong Xie*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线平台如何通过跨期需求分配影响卖家的库存选择，核心机制是信息性的：平台通过改变卖家销售流的可预测性来影响其安全库存需求，从而在不直接控制库存的情况下激励卖家选择平台履约服务（FBP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线市场平台的功能已超越简单的买卖匹配，扩展到订单路由和履约服务。平台希望卖家采用其履约服务（FBP）以获得更多收入，但无法直接控制卖家库存。因此，需要研究平台如何利用需求分配这一运营和信息设计杠杆来间接影响卖家的库存决策和履约选择。

Method: 建立博弈论模型，其中平台观察总需求并跨期分配订单给卖家；卖家在补货时采用状态依赖的基本库存策略，并选择商家自履约（FBM）或平台履约（FBP）两种方式。研究聚焦于非歧视性分配策略（给予卖家相同的需求份额和预测风险），分析不同分配规则如何改变卖家销售流的可预测性和安全库存需求。

Result: （1）均匀分配策略最小化预测不确定性；（2）任意更高水平的不确定性可通过简单的低记忆分配规则实现；（3）增加不确定性需要阻止卖家从自身销售历史推断总需求的路由规则；（4）平台的问题简化为选择预测不确定性水平，以权衡平台履约的采用率与采用者的库存持有成本。

Conclusion: 需求分配是数字市场中一个强大的运营和信息设计杠杆，平台可以通过改变销售流的可预测性来影响卖家的库存决策，即使平均需求份额保持不变。这为平台设计激励机制促进履约服务采用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Online marketplaces increasingly do more than simply match buyers and sellers: they route orders across competing sellers and, in many categories, offer ancillary fulfillment services that make seller inventory a source of platform revenue. We investigate how a platform can use intertemporal demand allocation to influence sellers' inventory choices without directly controlling stock. We develop a model in which the platform observes aggregate demand, allocates orders across sellers over time, and sellers choose between two fulfillment options, fulfill-by-merchant (FBM) and fulfill-by-platform (FBP), while replenishing inventory under state-dependent base-stock policies. The key mechanism we study is informational: by changing the predictability of each seller's sales stream, the platform changes sellers' safety-stock needs even when average demand shares remain unchanged. We focus on nondiscriminatory allocation policies that give sellers the same demand share and forecast risk. Within this class, uniform splitting minimizes forecast uncertainty, whereas any higher level of uncertainty can be implemented using simple low-memory allocation rules. Moreover, increasing uncertainty above the uniform benchmark requires routing rules that prevent sellers from inferring aggregate demand from their own sales histories. These results reduce the platform's problem to choosing a level of forecast uncertainty that trades off adoption of platform fulfillment against the inventory held by adopters. Our analysis identifies demand allocation as a powerful operational and informational design lever in digital marketplaces.

</details>
